====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: legacy/simulacoes_iniciais/simulacao_100_redes.py
SHA256: 8f6f54cc28b3f047f89ec0c133bd042b3d3458cb9ac4afcb5145cc40478299f9
====================================================================================================
"""Simulação reproduzível de 100 redes recorrentes, cada uma com 100 neurônios.

Em cada ciclo, toda rede envia seu vetor de 100 ativações às outras 99.
A atualização é síncrona; os pesos aleatórios ficam congelados (não há treino).
Execute: python simulacao_100_redes.py
Requisitos: numpy, matplotlib
"""
from pathlib import Path
import csv

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use("Agg")  # Gera o gráfico em arquivo, inclusive sem ambiente gráfico.
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np

QUANTIDADE_REDES = 100
NEURONIOS_POR_REDE = 100
CICLOS = 10
SEMENTE = 2026

# Fatores de influência na atualização de cada neurônio.
FORCA_INTERNA = 0.85
FORCA_COMUNICACAO = 0.90
FORCA_ESTIMULO_COMUM = 0.12
FORCA_VIES_INDIVIDUAL = 0.12

PASTA_SAIDA = Path(__file__).resolve().parent


def concordancia(estados: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """Cosseno médio entre os vetores de ativações de pares distintos."""
    comprimentos = np.linalg.norm(estados, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    unitarios = estados / np.maximum(comprimentos, 1e-12)
    similaridades = unitarios @ unitarios.T
    n = estados.shape[0]
    return float((similaridades.sum() - np.trace(similaridades)) / (n * (n - 1)))


def diversidade(estados: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """Distância RMS média, por neurônio, até o estado médio da população."""
    diferenca = estados - estados.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return float(np.sqrt(np.mean(diferenca**2)))


def simular(estados_iniciais, pesos, vieses, estimulo, comunicacao=True):
    """Devolve estados e métricas do instante inicial até o ciclo 10."""
    estados = estados_iniciais.copy()
    historico = [estados.copy()]
    metricas = []

    for ciclo in range(CICLOS + 1):
        metricas.append({
            "ciclo": ciclo,
            "concordancia": concordancia(estados),
            "diversidade": diversidade(estados),
            "amplitude_rms": float(np.sqrt(np.mean(estados**2))),
        })
        if ciclo == CICLOS:
            break

        # Produto interno: para cada rede, matriz 100x100 vezes vetor 100.
        processamento_interno = np.einsum("nij,nj->ni", pesos, estados)

        if comunicacao:
            # Cada rede recebe a média das ativações das OUTRAS 99 redes.
            # A fórmula compacta é exatamente equivalente a somar 99 mensagens.
            mensagens_das_outras = (
                estados.sum(axis=0, keepdims=True) - estados
            ) / (QUANTIDADE_REDES - 1)
        else:
            mensagens_das_outras = np.zeros_like(estados)

        # Atualização simultânea: nenhuma rede vê mensagens do ciclo seguinte.
        estados = np.tanh(
            FORCA_INTERNA * processamento_interno
            + FORCA_COMUNICACAO * mensagens_das_outras
            + FORCA_ESTIMULO_COMUM * estimulo
            + FORCA_VIES_INDIVIDUAL * vieses
        )
        historico.append(estados.copy())

    return np.asarray(historico), metricas


def main():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(SEMENTE)
    n, m = QUANTIDADE_REDES, NEURONIOS_POR_REDE

    # Cada rede tem pesos recorrentes próprios, fixos e aleatórios.
    pesos = rng.normal(0, 1 / np.sqrt(m), size=(n, m, m))
    estados_iniciais = rng.normal(0, 0.50, size=(n, m))
    vieses = rng.normal(0, 1, size=(n, m))
    estimulo = rng.normal(0, 1, size=(1, m))  # Mesmo estímulo para todas.

    hist_com, dados_com = simular(estados_iniciais, pesos, vieses, estimulo, True)
    hist_sem, dados_sem = simular(estados_iniciais, pesos, vieses, estimulo, False)

    arquivo_csv = PASTA_SAIDA / "metricas_100_redes.csv"
    with arquivo_csv.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as arquivo:
        escritor = csv.writer(arquivo)
        escritor.writerow([
            "ciclo", "concordancia_com", "concordancia_sem",
            "diversidade_com", "diversidade_sem", "rms_com", "rms_sem",
        ])
        for a, b in zip(dados_com, dados_sem):
            escritor.writerow([
                a["ciclo"], a["concordancia"], b["concordancia"],
                a["diversidade"], b["diversidade"],
                a["amplitude_rms"], b["amplitude_rms"],
            ])

    # Gráficos de métricas e de ativações finais de 20 redes.
    fig, axs = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(12, 8), constrained_layout=True)
    ciclos = [r["ciclo"] for r in dados_com]
    for nome, registros in [("Com comunicação", dados_com), ("Sem comunicação", dados_sem)]:
        axs[0, 0].plot(ciclos, [r["concordancia"] for r in registros], marker="o", label=nome)
        axs[0, 1].plot(ciclos, [r["diversidade"] for r in registros], marker="o", label=nome)
    axs[0, 0].set(title="Semelhança média entre pares", xlabel="Ciclo", ylabel="Cosseno médio", ylim=(-0.1, 1.05))
    axs[0, 1].set(title="Diversidade de estados", xlabel="Ciclo", ylabel="Desvio RMS por neurônio")
    for ax in axs[0]:
        ax.grid(alpha=0.25)
        ax.legend()

    for ax, matriz, titulo in [
        (axs[1, 0], hist_com[-1, :20], "Com comunicação — ciclo 10"),
        (axs[1, 1], hist_sem[-1, :20], "Sem comunicação — ciclo 10"),
    ]:
        im = ax.imshow(matriz, cmap="coolwarm", vmin=-0.8, vmax=0.8, aspect="auto")
        ax.set(title=titulo, xlabel="Neurônio (0–99)", ylabel="Rede (0–19)")
        fig.colorbar(im, ax=ax, shrink=0.75, label="Ativação")
    arquivo_png = PASTA_SAIDA / "resultado_100_redes.png"
    fig.savefig(arquivo_png, dpi=160)
    plt.close(fig)

    print("REDES:", n, "NEURÔNIOS/REDE:", m, "CICLOS:", CICLOS)
    print("Mensagens dirigidas por ciclo:", n * (n - 1))
    print("Mensagens dirigidas em 10 ciclos:", CICLOS * n * (n - 1))
    print("Valores neuronais transmitidos em 10 ciclos:", CICLOS * n * (n - 1) * m)
    print("\nCiclo | Concordância (com / sem) | Diversidade (com / sem)")
    for i in range(CICLOS + 1):
        a, b = dados_com[i], dados_sem[i]
        print(f"{i:>5} | {a['concordancia']:>8.4f} / {b['concordancia']:<8.4f} | {a['diversidade']:>8.4f} / {b['diversidade']:<8.4f}")
    print("\nArquivos:", arquivo_png, arquivo_csv, sep="\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: legacy/simulacoes_iniciais/simulacao_coletiva_1000.py
SHA256: 580c433a1f950a66ffe10283859bcb7e1a21ec34e29553726238d91fda887b0e
====================================================================================================
"""100 redes de 100 neuronios: deteccao coletiva de falhas correlacionadas.
Experimento sintético reprodutível, 1000 ciclos de aprendizado.

Instale: pip install numpy torch matplotlib
Execute: python simulacao_coletiva_1000.py
"""
import csv
import json
import random
from pathlib import Path

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

SEED = 20261017
NUM_NETWORKS = 100
NEURONS = 100
INPUT_DIM = 3
TRAIN_STEPS = 1000
BATCH_SIZE = 64
EVAL_SIZE = 4000
LEARNING_RATE = 0.008
ANOMALOUS_SENSORS = 30
COMMON_BACKGROUND = 0.12
CORRELATED_SIGNAL = 0.72
DEVICE = 'cpu'
OUT_DIR = Path('/mnt/data')

def setup():
    random.seed(SEED)
    np.random.seed(SEED)
    torch.manual_seed(SEED)
    torch.set_num_threads(4)


def make_batch(batch_size: int, generator: torch.Generator):
    """Constrói dados com a mesma distribuição local em ambas as classes.

    y=0: ruído comum de ambiente + ruído privado de cada sensor.
    y=1: o mesmo + um desvio sincronizado em 30 sensores aleatórios.
    Cada sensor, isoladamente, vê exatamente N(0,1) em seu canal principal.
    """
    b, n = batch_size, NUM_NETWORKS
    y = torch.randint(0, 2, (b,), generator=generator).float()
    common = torch.randn(b, 1, generator=generator)
    hidden_fault = torch.randn(b, 1, generator=generator)
    private = torch.randn(b, n, generator=generator)

    # Seleção aleatória de 30 nós distintos por exemplo, somente classe positiva.
    ranking = torch.rand(b, n, generator=generator).argsort(dim=1)
    chosen = torch.zeros(b, n)
    chosen.scatter_(1, ranking[:, :ANOMALOUS_SENSORS], 1.0)
    chosen *= y[:, None]
    correlated_var = CORRELATED_SIGNAL * chosen
    # Variância total exatamente 1, independentemente do rótulo.
    main = (COMMON_BACKGROUND**0.5)*common + (correlated_var**0.5)*hidden_fault + (1-COMMON_BACKGROUND-correlated_var).sqrt()*private

    # Referência ambiental: captura parcialmente o fator comum sem indicar o rótulo.
    environment = (0.65**0.5)*common + (0.35**0.5)*torch.randn(b, n, generator=generator)
    distractor = torch.randn(b, n, generator=generator)
    x = torch.stack((main, environment, distractor), dim=-1)
    return x, y


class Collective(nn.Module):
    """100 redes independentes com 100 neurônios tanh cada uma.

    Cada rede envia seu vetor de 100 ativações a todas as outras 99.
    Receptores agregam a média das 99 mensagens, depois calculam seu voto.
    As matrizes e as portas são particulares de cada rede e aprendidas.
    """
    def __init__(self, communicate: bool):
        super().__init__()
        self.communicate = communicate
        shared = torch.randn(INPUT_DIM, NEURONS) * 0.16
        self.encoder = nn.Parameter(shared[None].expand(NUM_NETWORKS,-1,-1).clone() + torch.randn(NUM_NETWORKS,INPUT_DIM,NEURONS)*0.02)
        self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(NUM_NETWORKS, NEURONS))
        self.local_gate = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(NUM_NETWORKS,NEURONS))
        self.msg_gate = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(NUM_NETWORKS,NEURONS)*0.8)
        self.interaction_gate = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(NUM_NETWORKS,NEURONS)*0.7)
        self.hidden_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(NUM_NETWORKS,NEURONS))
        self.readout = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(NUM_NETWORKS,NEURONS)*0.04)
        self.readout_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(NUM_NETWORKS))
        self.output_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(0.0))

    def forward(self, x, return_activity=False):
        h = torch.tanh(torch.einsum('bni,nih->bnh', x, self.encoder) + self.bias)
        if self.communicate:
            msg = (h.sum(dim=1,keepdim=True)-h)/(NUM_NETWORKS-1)
        else:
            msg = torch.zeros_like(h)
        # Coordenação neuronal mediante mensagens dos 99 outros agentes.
        combined = (self.local_gate*h + self.msg_gate*msg + self.interaction_gate*(h*msg)+self.hidden_bias)
        state = torch.tanh(combined)
        local_votes = (state*self.readout).sum(dim=-1)/NEURONS**0.5+self.readout_bias
        collective_logit = local_votes.mean(dim=1) + self.output_bias
        if return_activity:
            return collective_logit, h.detach(), state.detach()
        return collective_logit


def evaluate(models, x, labels):
    outputs={}
    for name,model in models.items():
        model.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            scores=torch.cat([model(xi).sigmoid() for xi in x.split(250)],dim=0).numpy()
        y=labels.numpy().astype(int)
        pred=scores>=0.5
        accuracy=float((pred==y).mean())
        pos=scores[y==1];neg=scores[y==0]
        # AUC por ranks, com tratamento de empates via ranks scipy-free.
        order=np.argsort(scores,kind='mergesort')
        sorted_scores=scores[order]
        ranks=np.empty(len(scores),dtype=float)
        j=0
        while j<len(scores):
            k=j+1
            while k<len(scores) and sorted_scores[k]==sorted_scores[j]:k+=1
            ranks[order[j:k]]=(j+1+k)/2
            j=k
        auc=(ranks[y==1].sum()-len(pos)*(len(pos)+1)/2)/(len(pos)*len(neg))
        outputs[name]={'accuracy':accuracy,'auc':float(auc),'mean_positive_score':float(pos.mean()),'mean_negative_score':float(neg.mean()),'scores':scores}
    return outputs


def main():
    setup()
    g_train=torch.Generator().manual_seed(SEED+1)
    g_test=torch.Generator().manual_seed(SEED+2)
    x_test,y_test=make_batch(EVAL_SIZE,g_test)
    connected=Collective(True)
    standalone=Collective(False)
    standalone.load_state_dict(connected.state_dict())
    models={'Com comunicação':connected,'Sem comunicação':standalone}
    optimizers={name:torch.optim.Adam(model.parameters(),lr=LEARNING_RATE) for name,model in models.items()}
    logs=[]
    for step in range(1,TRAIN_STEPS+1):
        x,y=make_batch(BATCH_SIZE,g_train)
        row={'ciclo':step}
        for name,model in models.items():
            model.train()
            optimizer=optimizers[name]
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            logits=model(x)
            loss=F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(logits,y)
            loss.backward()
            torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(),max_norm=5)
            optimizer.step()
            key='com' if model.communicate else 'sem'
            row['perda_'+key]=round(loss.item(),6)
            row['acerto_treino_'+key]=round(((logits.detach()>=0)==y.bool()).float().mean().item(),6)
        logs.append(row)
        if step in (1,50,100,200,300,500,700,1000):
            print(f'ciclo={step:4d} loss_com={row["perda_com"]:.4f} loss_sem={row["perda_sem"]:.4f}',flush=True)
    results=evaluate(models,x_test,y_test)
    serial={name:{k:v for k,v in r.items() if k!='scores'} for name,r in results.items()}
    print('RESULTADOS',json.dumps(serial,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),flush=True)
    # Confirma que cada rede possui 100 neurônios e recebe a média exata de 99 outras.
    with torch.no_grad():
        _,h,s=connected(x_test[:8],True)
    assert h.shape==(8,100,100) and s.shape==(8,100,100)
    fast=(h.sum(1,keepdim=True)-h)/99
    slow=torch.stack([h[:,torch.arange(100)!=i,:].mean(1) for i in range(100)],dim=1)
    assert torch.allclose(fast,slow,atol=1e-6)
    # Avaliação de incerteza: bootstrap pareado de acurácia e AUC.
    rng=np.random.default_rng(SEED+3)
    yc=y_test.numpy().astype(bool)
    c=results['Com comunicação']['scores'];s=results['Sem comunicação']['scores']
    differences=[]
    for _ in range(1000):
        idx=rng.integers(0,len(yc),size=len(yc))
        differences.append(((c[idx]>=0.5)==yc[idx]).mean()-((s[idx]>=0.5)==yc[idx]).mean())
    serial['comparacao']={'delta_acuracia':results['Com comunicação']['accuracy']-results['Sem comunicação']['accuracy'],
                          'ic95_bootstrap_delta_acuracia':[float(q) for q in np.percentile(differences,[2.5,97.5])],
                          'total_ciclos':TRAIN_STEPS,'amostras_teste':EVAL_SIZE,'mensagens_dirigidas_por_ciclo':9900,'valores_transmitidos_por_ciclo_sem_lote':990000,
                          'mensagens_dirigidas_durante_treino':9900000,'observacao':'Contagens por exemplo; durante treinamento com lote de 64 multiplicar pelo tamanho do lote.'}
    (OUT_DIR/'resultados_1000_ciclos.json').write_text(json.dumps(serial,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False),encoding='utf-8')
    with (OUT_DIR/'treinamento_1000_ciclos.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
        writer=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(logs[0]));writer.writeheader();writer.writerows(logs)
    np.savez_compressed(OUT_DIR/'teste_1000_ciclos.npz',rotulos=y_test.numpy(),prob_comunicacao=c,prob_sem_comunicacao=s)
    torch.save({'config':{'redes':NUM_NETWORKS,'neuronios':NEURONS,'ciclos':TRAIN_STEPS,'semente':SEED},'com':connected.state_dict(),'sem':standalone.state_dict()},OUT_DIR/'pesos_1000_ciclos.pt')
    plt.rcParams.update({'font.size':10,'axes.spines.top':False,'axes.spines.right':False})
    fig,axes=plt.subplots(2,2,figsize=(12.7,8.2),layout='constrained')
    t=np.array([r['ciclo'] for r in logs]);win=25
    for name,color,suf in [('Com comunicação','#146fa8','com'),('Sem comunicação','#dd8041','sem')]:
        losses=np.array([r['perda_'+suf] for r in logs]);accuracy=np.array([r['acerto_treino_'+suf] for r in logs])
        smooth=lambda v:np.convolve(np.pad(v,(win-1,0),mode='edge'),np.ones(win)/win,mode='valid')
        axes[0,0].plot(t,smooth(losses),label=name,color=color)
        axes[0,1].plot(t,smooth(accuracy),label=name,color=color)
    axes[0,0].set(title='Erro durante os 1.000 ciclos',xlabel='Ciclo de treinamento',ylabel='Entropia cruzada (menor = melhor)')
    axes[0,1].set(title='Acerto de treinamento (média móvel: 25)',xlabel='Ciclo de treinamento',ylabel='Proporção de acertos',ylim=(0.3,1.02))
    axes[0,0].legend()
    names=list(models)
    xs=np.arange(2)
    axes[1,0].bar(xs-0.16,[results[n]['accuracy'] for n in names],width=.32,label='Acurácia',color='#277ca7')
    axes[1,0].bar(xs+0.16,[results[n]['auc'] for n in names],width=.32,label='AUC',color='#8b6bb4')
    axes[1,0].set(xticks=xs,xticklabels=['Com comunicação','Sem comunicação'],ylim=(0,1),title=f'Teste independente: {EVAL_SIZE:,} exemplos'.replace(',','.'),ylabel='Resultado (maior = melhor)')
    axes[1,0].legend()
    axes[1,1].hist(c[~yc],bins=np.linspace(0,1,36),alpha=.7,label='Normal',color='#277ca7',density=True)
    axes[1,1].hist(c[yc],bins=np.linspace(0,1,36),alpha=.55,label='Falha coordenada',color='#d98243',density=True)
    axes[1,1].set(title='Probabilidades da rede coletiva no teste',xlabel='Probabilidade prevista de falha',ylabel='Densidade')
    axes[1,1].legend()
    fig.suptitle('100 redes × 100 neurônios × 1.000 ciclos | detecção distribuída de falhas',fontsize=14,fontweight='bold')
    fig.savefig(OUT_DIR/'grafico_1000_ciclos.png',dpi=165)
    plt.close(fig)
    print('Verificações: dimensões e equivalência entre mensagens explícitas/otimizadas OK',flush=True)

if __name__=='__main__':main()

"""Teste independente: remover mensagens após os 1000 ciclos de treinamento.
Execute depois do simulacao_coletiva_1000.py.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import torch
from simulacao_coletiva_1000 import Collective, make_batch, EVAL_SIZE, SEED

root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
checkpoint = torch.load(root/'pesos_1000_ciclos.pt', map_location='cpu', weights_only=True)
network = Collective(communicate=True)
network.load_state_dict(checkpoint['com'])
network.eval()

# Reconstrói os mesmos exemplos de teste independentes do treinamento.
x, y = make_batch(EVAL_SIZE, torch.Generator().manual_seed(SEED+2))
with torch.no_grad():
    prediction_connected = network(x).sigmoid()
    network.communicate = False  # Mantém os pesos, mas remove as mensagens.
    prediction_blocked = network(x).sigmoid()

accuracy = lambda output: ((output >= 0.5) == y.bool()).float().mean().item()
print('Com comunicação: {:.2%}'.format(accuracy(prediction_connected)))
print('Após bloquear mensagens: {:.2%}'.format(accuracy(prediction_blocked)))

====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nexus_m/alignment_and_value_core.py
SHA256: 01e1e20769c94d968b69bd71cc93a0b9d84dea04eba233b54a32cd811441267a

"""Enforceable local operating constraints and tamper-evident action ledger.

This module is NOT a solution to AI alignment or a proof of human values. It
provides bounded permissions, reproducible audits, manual promotion gates,
resource quotas and explicit denials. External process isolation requires
separate OS/container controls; a Python policy cannot be incorruptible.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass,asdict
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib,json,os,time

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ResourcePolicy:
    max_mcts_simulations:int=100_000
    max_mcts_depth:int=1024
    max_agents:int=1000
    max_neurons_per_agent:int=10_000
    max_parameters:int=25_000_000
    max_disk_bytes:int=2_000_000_000
    max_wall_seconds:float=3600.
    allow_network:bool=False
    allow_cloud_provision:bool=False
    allow_native_code_execution:bool=False
    auto_promote_source_changes:bool=False

@dataclass
class Decision:
    allowed:bool
    action:str
    reason:str
    needs_approval:bool=False
    def as_dict(self):return asdict(self)


class AlignmentGuard:
    """Local capability guard, not a value-learning or formal safety theorem."""
    def __init__(self,workspace:str|Path,policy:ResourcePolicy|None=None):
        self.workspace=Path(workspace).resolve()
        self.workspace.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        self.policy=policy or ResourcePolicy()
        self.audit=self.workspace/'audit_chain.jsonl'
        self._last_hash=self._read_last_hash()

    def _read_last_hash(self):
        prev='0'*64
        if not self.audit.exists():return prev
        for line in self.audit.read_text(encoding='utf-8').splitlines():
            record=json.loads(line)
            payload={k:v for k,v in record.items() if k!='hash'}
            actual=hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(payload,sort_keys=True,ensure_ascii=False).encode()).hexdigest()
            if payload['prev_hash']!=prev or actual!=record['hash']:
                raise RuntimeError('audit history inconsistency')
            prev=actual
        return prev

    def _log(self,decision:Decision,details:dict|None=None):
        entry={'timestamp':time.time(),'decision':decision.as_dict(),'details':details or {},
               'prev_hash':self._last_hash}
        entry['hash']=hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(entry,sort_keys=True,ensure_ascii=False).encode()).hexdigest()
        with self.audit.open('a',encoding='utf-8') as f:f.write(json.dumps(entry,ensure_ascii=False,sort_keys=True)+'\n')
        self._last_hash=entry['hash']

    def authorize(self,action:str,details:dict|None=None,approved:bool=False)->Decision:
        d=details or {};p=self.policy
        if action=='mcts':
            valid=(1<=int(d.get('simulations',0))<=p.max_mcts_simulations and
                   1<=int(d.get('depth',0))<=p.max_mcts_depth)
            decision=Decision(valid,action,'within search budget' if valid else 'search budget exceeded')
        elif action=='topology':
            valid=(2<=int(d.get('agents',0))<=p.max_agents and
                   4<=int(d.get('width',0))<=p.max_neurons_per_agent and
                   int(d.get('parameters',0))<=p.max_parameters)
            decision=Decision(valid,action,'within topology budget' if valid else 'topology budget exceeded')
        elif action=='network':
            decision=Decision(p.allow_network and approved,action,
                              'network access requires configuration and approval',True)
        elif action=='cloud_provision':
            decision=Decision(p.allow_cloud_provision and approved,action,
                              'cloud provisioning requires opt-in and human approval',True)
        elif action=='native_execute':
            decision=Decision(p.allow_native_code_execution and approved,action,
                              'native execution requires explicit permissions',True)
        elif action in ('source_edit','hot_swap'):
            try:
                target=(self.workspace/str(d.get('relative_path',''))).resolve()
                relative=target.relative_to(self.workspace)
                valid=len(relative.parts)>=2 and relative.parts[0]=='managed' and relative.suffix=='.py'
            except ValueError:valid=False
            allow=valid and (approved or p.auto_promote_source_changes)
            decision=Decision(allow,action,
                  'allowlisted managed source and promotion approval required',not allow and valid)
        elif action=='disk_write':
            valid=0<=int(d.get('bytes',-1))<=p.max_disk_bytes
            decision=Decision(valid,action,'disk write budget' if valid else 'disk budget exceeded')
        else:
            decision=Decision(False,action,'unknown capability')
        self._log(decision,d)
        return decision

    def verify_ledger(self)->bool:
        self._read_last_hash()
        return True

    def invariant_report(self)->dict:
        return {'enforced': ['workspace-limited source promotion','manual gate by default',
                             'bounded search','bounded topology','audit hash chain'],
                'not_guaranteed':['human value alignment','recursive safety proofs',
                                  'hostile process containment','tamper resistance against OS administrators'],
                'policy':asdict(self.policy),'audit_integrity':self.verify_ledger()}


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nexus_m/ast_candidate_runtime.py
SHA256: c5bf2fa96900d49650f29ce027abd4d9a94dbda675c430f6477102401936f014
====================================================================================================
"""Validate generated Python AST, measure candidates inside bounded child processes."""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass,asdict
from pathlib import Path
import json,math,random,subprocess,sys
from .ast_program_synthesis import RouterSpec,compile_source,validate_generated_source,reference_route

@dataclass
class CandidateMeasurement:
    spec:dict
    equivalent:bool
    baseline_ms:float|None
    candidate_ms:float|None
    improvement:float
    error:str|None
    mismatches:list
    def as_dict(self):return asdict(self)


def make_cases(seed:int=2026,checks:int=65,timing:int=45,large:bool=False):
    rng=random.Random(seed)
    cases=[]
    for v,k in [([],0),([],1),([0.],0),([0.],1),([1.,1.,0.],2),
                ([0.,1.,1.,1.],3),([5.,5.,5.,5.],4),([-4.,-5.,-3.],1),
                ([1.,2.],-1),([1.,2.],3),([2.0,-2.0,1.0],1)]:
        cases.append([v,k])
    for _ in range(checks):
        n=rng.choice([0,1,2,3,5,8,12,32,64,128])
        scores=[float(rng.choice([-3,-2,-1,0,1,2,3]) if rng.random()<.45
                        else round(rng.uniform(-5,5),4)) for _ in range(n)]
        k=rng.choice([0,1,2,3,n,max(0,n-1),n+1,-1])
        cases.append([scores,k])
    benchmarking=[]
    for _ in range(timing):
        n=rng.choice([400,800,1600,3200] if large else [120,300,600,1200])
        scores=[rng.uniform(-100,100) for _ in range(n)]
        benchmarking.append([scores,rng.choice([1,2,4,8])])
    return cases,benchmarking


def _set_limits():
    # Linux/Unix defense-in-depth. Not a substitute for an OS sandbox.
    try:
        import resource
        resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_CPU,(6,7))
        resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_AS,(512*1024**2,512*1024**2))
        resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_FSIZE,(8*1024**2,8*1024**2))
    except (ImportError,OSError,ValueError):pass


def evaluate(spec:RouterSpec,*,seed:int=2026,large:bool=False,
             timeout:float=8.0,checks:int=65,timing:int=45,repetitions:int=3,
             baseline_spec:RouterSpec|None=None)->CandidateMeasurement:
    source=compile_source(spec)
    validate_generated_source(source)
    base=compile_source(baseline_spec or RouterSpec())
    check_cases,timing_cases=make_cases(seed,checks,timing,large)
    payload=json.dumps({'baseline':base,'candidate':source,'checks':check_cases,
                        'timing':timing_cases,'repetitions':repetitions})
    try:
        result=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-I',str(Path(__file__).with_name('ast_runtime_harness.py'))],
                 input=payload,text=True,capture_output=True,timeout=timeout,
                 cwd=Path(__file__).parent,preexec_fn=_set_limits if sys.platform!='win32' else None)
        if result.returncode!=0:
            raise RuntimeError('child exit '+str(result.returncode)+': '+result.stderr[-500:])
        data=json.loads(result.stdout)
        a,b=data.get('baseline_ms'),data.get('candidate_ms')
        gain=(a-b)/a if a and b is not None and a>0 else 0.
        return CandidateMeasurement(spec.as_dict(),data['equivalent'],a,b,gain,None,data['mismatches'])
    except (subprocess.TimeoutExpired,ValueError,RuntimeError,json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        return CandidateMeasurement(spec.as_dict(),False,None,None,0.,str(exc),[])


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nexus_m/ast_formal_gate.py
SHA256: dd74619595620241b99dbebf853be3d73e4242724d88eb105e99ea9c2046db0d

"""Evidence gate for synthesized router programs.

Provides a *construction/termination argument* and exhaustive equivalence on a
finite domain. This is NOT a machine-checked Lean/Coq proof of arbitrary Python
or a general guarantee of memory safety. Fail closed for unsupported templates.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass,asdict
import heapq,itertools
from .ast_program_synthesis import RouterSpec,compile_source,validate_generated_source,reference_route

@dataclass
class VerificationEvidence:
    canonical_ast:bool
    bounded_loops:bool
    finite_domain_equivalent:bool
    checked_cases:int
    checker_name:str
    machine_checked_universal_proof:bool
    statement:str
    def as_dict(self):return asdict(self)


def verify(spec:RouterSpec,values=(-2.0,-1.0,0.0,1.0),max_n:int=4)->VerificationEvidence:
    if not 0<=max_n<=6: raise ValueError('max_n must be in 0..6')
    source=compile_source(spec)
    canonical=validate_generated_source(source)==spec
    bounded=spec.validation=='strict' and spec.strategy in ('sort','heap','scan')
    # This exec is exclusively over canonical program AST produced by our DSL.
    env={'__builtins__':{'range':range,'len':len,'sorted':sorted,'min':min,
                          'list':list,'ValueError':ValueError},'heapq':heapq}
    exec(compile(source,'<canonical_router_ast>','exec'),env)
    fn=env['route']
    count=0;passed=True
    for n in range(max_n+1):
        for scores in itertools.product(values,repeat=n):
            for k in range(-1,n+2):
                count+=1
                a=list(scores)
                try: expected=('ok',reference_route(a,k))
                except Exception as exc:expected=('error',type(exc).__name__)
                try: observed=('ok',fn(a,k))
                except Exception as exc:observed=('error',type(exc).__name__)
                if expected!=observed:
                    passed=False;break
            if not passed:break
        if not passed:break
    return VerificationEvidence(canonical,bounded,passed,count,
          'finite_exhaustive_and_structural_reasoning',False,
          'Exhaustive over finite inputs only. Structural termination follows from '
          'bounded list primitives or a finite range(k) under the strict k guard; '
          'no universal formal theorem proof has been generated.')


def promotion_eligible(proof:VerificationEvidence)->bool:
    return proof.canonical_ast and proof.bounded_loops and proof.finite_domain_equivalent


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nexus_m/ast_mcts.py
SHA256: e19da06ef1ad64857992ed19699dde5973fd134494a2c233f3b94d3d9c8e75c0
====================================================================================================
"""Monte Carlo Tree Search over compositional AST-construction decisions.

Search nodes represent *partial typed program sketches*, not string fragments.
Each complete sketch becomes genuine Python AST; correctness and speed are
measured by the child-process evaluator. No LLM is involved.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass,field,asdict
from pathlib import Path
import json,math,random,time
from .ast_program_synthesis import FIELDS,RouterSpec,compile_source
from .ast_candidate_runtime import CandidateMeasurement,evaluate

@dataclass
class _Node:
    choices:tuple[str,...]=()
    visits:int=0
    total_reward:float=0.
    children:dict[str,'_Node']=field(default_factory=dict)
    @property
    def mean(self):return self.total_reward/self.visits if self.visits else 0.

@dataclass
class SynthesisReport:
    simulations:int
    unique_candidates:int
    accepted_candidates:int
    best_spec:dict
    best_train_gain:float
    elapsed_seconds:float
    trajectory:list[dict]
    candidates:list[dict]
    best_source:str
    def as_dict(self):return asdict(self)

class ASTSynthesisMCTS:
    def __init__(self,simulations=96,seed=2026,exploration=1.7,
                 checks=45,timing=22,timeout=8.0,baseline_spec:RouterSpec|None=None):
        if not 1<=simulations<=500:raise ValueError('synthesis budget 1..500')
        self.simulations=simulations;self.rng=random.Random(seed)
        self.seed=seed;self.exploration=exploration
        self.checks=checks;self.timing=timing;self.timeout=timeout
        self.baseline_spec=baseline_spec or RouterSpec()
        self.cache:dict[RouterSpec,CandidateMeasurement]={}
        self.root=_Node()

    def _select(self):
        node=self.root;path=[node]
        while len(node.choices)<len(FIELDS):
            name,options=FIELDS[len(node.choices)]
            unexpanded=[v for v in options if v not in node.children]
            if unexpanded:
                chosen=self.rng.choice(unexpanded)
                child=_Node(node.choices+(chosen,))
                node.children[chosen]=child
                node=child;path.append(node)
                break
            parent_visits=max(1,node.visits)
            node=max(node.children.values(),key=lambda c:
                (c.mean+self.exploration*math.sqrt(math.log(parent_visits+1)/(c.visits+1))
                 + self.rng.random()*1e-8))
            path.append(node)
        choices=list(node.choices)
        while len(choices)<len(FIELDS):
            choices.append(self.rng.choice(FIELDS[len(choices)][1]))
        return RouterSpec(*choices),path

    @staticmethod
    def _reward(m:CandidateMeasurement)->float:
        if not m.equivalent:return -1.
        return 0.12+max(-0.9,min(0.88,m.improvement))

    def search(self,*,max_unique:int=24)->SynthesisReport:
        started=time.perf_counter();trajectory=[]
        best=RouterSpec()
        best_gain=-1e20
        for t in range(self.simulations):
            spec,path=self._select()
            if spec not in self.cache and len(self.cache)<max_unique:
                self.cache[spec]=evaluate(spec,seed=self.seed+11,checks=self.checks,
                            timing=self.timing,timeout=self.timeout,baseline_spec=self.baseline_spec)
            # Once unique budget is exhausted, replay only cached results.
            m=self.cache.get(spec)
            reward=self._reward(m) if m else -1.
            for n in path:
                n.visits+=1;n.total_reward+=reward
            if m and m.equivalent and m.improvement>best_gain:
                best_gain=m.improvement;best=spec
            trajectory.append({'simulation':t+1,'candidate':spec.as_dict(),
                    'cached':m is not None,'reward':reward,
                    'best_gain':best_gain if best_gain>-1e10 else None,
                    'unique_evaluated':len(self.cache)})
        # In case no candidate passed, return canonical baseline.
        if best_gain==-1e20:
            best=RouterSpec();best_gain=0.
        return SynthesisReport(self.simulations,len(self.cache),
            sum(m.equivalent for m in self.cache.values()),best.as_dict(),
            best_gain,time.perf_counter()-started,trajectory,
            [m.as_dict() for m in self.cache.values()],compile_source(best))

ARQUIVO: nexus_m/ast_performance_profiler.py
SHA256: 6c2dd9548a1adf0992a948bd5491f19e755b48a8385702a03a16544e003d1cc9

"""Profile the managed router to create reproducible bottleneck observations."""
from __future__ import annotations
import cProfile,pstats,io,random,time
from dataclasses import dataclass,asdict
from .ast_program_synthesis import RouterSpec,compile_source
from .search_driven_improver import SearchDrivenImprover

@dataclass
class ProfileReport:
    calls:int
    total_ms:float
    mean_microseconds:float
    hotspots:list[dict]
    recommendation:str
    def as_dict(self):return asdict(self)

def profile(improver:SearchDrivenImprover,*,iterations:int=100,seed:int=2026)->ProfileReport:
    if not 1<=iterations<=10000:raise ValueError('iterations must be bounded')
    rng=random.Random(seed)
    batches=[([rng.uniform(-10.,10.) for _ in range(rng.choice((256,1000,2000)))],4)
             for _ in range(iterations)]
    profiler=cProfile.Profile()
    t=time.perf_counter()
    profiler.enable()
    for scores,k in batches:improver.route(scores,k)
    profiler.disable()
    elapsed=(time.perf_counter()-t)*1000
    stats=pstats.Stats(profiler)
    rows=[]
    for (filename,line,func),(cc,nc,total,cumulative,callers) in stats.stats.items():
        if func in ('route','sorted','nsmallest','<lambda>','min'):
            rows.append({'function':func,'calls':nc,
                        'self_ms':total*1000,'cumulative_ms':cumulative*1000})
    rows.sort(key=lambda x:x['cumulative_ms'],reverse=True)
    recommendation=('Try top-k selective search (heap or selection) rather than complete sorting'
                    if any(r['function']=='sorted' for r in rows) else
                    'Benchmark alternative stable tie-breaking and indexing')
    return ProfileReport(iterations,elapsed,elapsed*1000/iterations,rows[:12],recommendation)


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nexus_m/ast_program_synthesis.py
SHA256: 2b737937b96119d172b04d3d7f9d18ccde36209887888e8931e6c889fff88577
====================================================================================================
"""Compositional AST synthesis for a deterministic top-k routing kernel.

Not unrestricted Python synthesis: the search space is a typed, auditable DSL
compiled into actual Python AST. Every produced program terminates, has
bounded loops (for finite inputs) and accesses only an explicitly checked API.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import ast
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from typing import Iterator

STRATEGIES = ('sort', 'heap', 'scan', 'reverse_sort')
KEYS = ('stable', 'score', 'index')
VALIDATIONS = ('strict', 'omitted')
FIELDS = (('strategy', STRATEGIES), ('key', KEYS), ('validation', VALIDATIONS))

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RouterSpec:
    strategy: str = 'sort'
    key: str = 'stable'
    validation: str = 'strict'
    def __post_init__(self):
        for name, options in FIELDS:
            if getattr(self, name) not in options:
                raise ValueError(f'{name} must be one of {options}')
    def as_dict(self): return asdict(self)


def _name(s: str, ctx: ast.expr_context | None = None) -> ast.Name:
    return ast.Name(id=s, ctx=ctx or ast.Load())


def _call(fn: ast.expr, *args: ast.expr) -> ast.Call:
    return ast.Call(func=fn, args=list(args), keywords=[])


def _rank(key: str) -> ast.expr:
    i = _name('i')
    score = ast.Subscript(value=_name('scores'), slice=i, ctx=ast.Load())
    if key == 'stable':
        expr = ast.Tuple(elts=[ast.UnaryOp(op=ast.USub(), operand=score), i], ctx=ast.Load())
    elif key == 'score':
        expr = ast.UnaryOp(op=ast.USub(), operand=score)
    else:
        expr = i
    return ast.Lambda(
        args=ast.arguments(posonlyargs=[], args=[ast.arg(arg='i')],
                           vararg=None, kwonlyargs=[], kw_defaults=[], kwarg=None, defaults=[]),
        body=expr,
    )


def _range_indices() -> ast.Call:
    return _call(_name('range'), _call(_name('len'), _name('scores')))


def build_router_ast(spec: RouterSpec) -> ast.Module:
    """Create syntax nodes structurally; no static source-code templates."""
    stmts: list[ast.stmt] = []
    if spec.validation == 'strict':
        compare = ast.Compare(left=ast.Constant(0), ops=[ast.LtE(), ast.LtE()],
                              comparators=[_name('k'), _call(_name('len'), _name('scores'))])
        stmts.append(ast.If(test=ast.UnaryOp(op=ast.Not(), operand=compare),
                            body=[ast.Raise(exc=_call(_name('ValueError'), ast.Constant('invalid k')),
                                            cause=None)], orelse=[]))
    if spec.strategy == 'scan':
        stmts.extend([
            ast.Assign(targets=[_name('remaining', ast.Store())], value=_call(_name('list'), _range_indices())),
            ast.Assign(targets=[_name('chosen', ast.Store())], value=ast.List(elts=[], ctx=ast.Load())),
            ast.For(target=_name('_', ast.Store()), iter=_call(_name('range'), _name('k')),
                    body=[
                        ast.Assign(targets=[_name('i', ast.Store())],
                                   value=ast.Call(func=_name('min'), args=[_name('remaining')],
                                                  keywords=[ast.keyword(arg='key', value=_rank(spec.key))])),
                        ast.Expr(value=_call(ast.Attribute(value=_name('chosen'), attr='append', ctx=ast.Load()), _name('i'))),
                        ast.Expr(value=_call(ast.Attribute(value=_name('remaining'), attr='remove', ctx=ast.Load()), _name('i'))),
                    ], orelse=[]),
            ast.Return(value=_name('chosen')),
        ])
    else:
        indices = _range_indices()
        if spec.strategy == 'heap':
            selection = ast.Call(func=ast.Attribute(value=_name('heapq'), attr='nsmallest', ctx=ast.Load()),
                                 args=[_name('k'), indices],
                                 keywords=[ast.keyword(arg='key', value=_rank(spec.key))])
        elif spec.strategy == 'sort':
            selection = ast.Subscript(
                value=ast.Call(func=_name('sorted'), args=[indices],
                               keywords=[ast.keyword(arg='key', value=_rank(spec.key))]),
                slice=ast.Slice(lower=None, upper=_name('k'), step=None), ctx=ast.Load())
        else:
            # reverse-sort also produces valid ranked results, but is intentionally inefficient.
            ordered = ast.Call(func=_name('sorted'), args=[indices],
                               keywords=[ast.keyword(arg='key', value=_rank(spec.key)),
                                         ast.keyword(arg='reverse', value=ast.Constant(True))])
            selection = ast.Subscript(value=ast.Subscript(value=ordered,
                                    slice=ast.Slice(lower=None, upper=ast.UnaryOp(op=ast.USub(), operand=_name('k')),
                                                    step=None),ctx=ast.Load()),
                                      slice=ast.Slice(lower=None,upper=None,
                                                      step=ast.UnaryOp(op=ast.USub(),operand=ast.Constant(1))),
                                      ctx=ast.Load())
        stmts.append(ast.Return(value=selection))
    fn = ast.FunctionDef(name='route',
          args=ast.arguments(posonlyargs=[],args=[ast.arg(arg='scores'),ast.arg(arg='k')],
                vararg=None,kwonlyargs=[],kw_defaults=[],kwarg=None,defaults=[]),
          body=stmts,decorator_list=[])
    return ast.fix_missing_locations(ast.Module(body=[fn], type_ignores=[]))


def compile_source(spec: RouterSpec) -> str:
    tree = build_router_ast(spec)
    compile(tree, '<generated_ast>', 'exec')
    return ast.unparse(tree) + '\n'


def validate_generated_source(source: str) -> RouterSpec:
    """Only accept exact normalized ASTs emitted by this generator."""
    try: actual = ast.dump(ast.parse(source), include_attributes=False)
    except SyntaxError as exc: raise ValueError('candidate cannot be parsed') from exc
    for a in STRATEGIES:
        for b in KEYS:
            for c in VALIDATIONS:
                spec = RouterSpec(a,b,c)
                if ast.dump(build_router_ast(spec),include_attributes=False)==actual:
                    return spec
    raise ValueError('source is not a canonical AST in the approved search grammar')


def all_router_specs() -> Iterator[RouterSpec]:
    for strategy in STRATEGIES:
        for key in KEYS:
            for validation in VALIDATIONS:
                yield RouterSpec(strategy,key,validation)


def reference_route(scores: list[float],k: int)->list[int]:
    if not 0 <= k <= len(scores): raise ValueError('invalid k')
    return sorted(range(len(scores)), key=lambda i:(-scores[i],i))[:k]


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nexus_m/ast_runtime_harness.py
SHA256: b361a1467263fc2e778981fe7128a70f978431c6c192c2dad825d903c6e3c64f
====================================================================================================
"""Child-process driver. Never run this module against arbitrary sources.

Parent validates exact canonical ASTs. This child has tight builtins, and the
parent applies OS limits/timeouts. It is *not* a hostile-code security boundary.
"""
import heapq
import json
import statistics
import sys
import time

SAFE_BUILTINS = {'sorted': sorted, 'range':range, 'len':len,
                 'ValueError':ValueError, 'min':min, 'list':list}

def _fn(source):
    namespace={'__builtins__':SAFE_BUILTINS,'heapq':heapq}
    exec(compile(source, '<approved_ast>', 'exec'), namespace)
    return namespace['route']

def _outcome(fn,scores,k):
    try:return ['ok',fn(scores,k)]
    except Exception as exc:return ['error',type(exc).__name__]

def _time(fn,cases,repetitions):
    for vals,k in cases[:8]:fn(vals,k)
    samples=[]
    for _ in range(repetitions):
        t=time.perf_counter_ns()
        for vals,k in cases:fn(vals,k)
        samples.append((time.perf_counter_ns()-t)/1e6)
    return statistics.median(samples)

def main():
    payload=json.load(sys.stdin)
    baseline=_fn(payload['baseline']);candidate=_fn(payload['candidate'])
    mismatches=[]
    for i,(values,k) in enumerate(payload['checks']):
        a,b=_outcome(baseline,values,k),_outcome(candidate,values,k)
        if a!=b:
            mismatches.append({'case':i,'expected':a,'actual':b})
            if len(mismatches)>=4:break
    if mismatches:
        print(json.dumps({'equivalent':False,'mismatches':mismatches,'baseline_ms':None,'candidate_ms':None}))
        return
    # Two timings executed in same child, with balanced ordering to limit clock drift.
    cases=payload['timing'];repeat=payload.get('repetitions',3)
    baseline_ms=_time(baseline,cases,repeat)
    candidate_ms=_time(candidate,cases,repeat)
    print(json.dumps({'equivalent':True,'mismatches':[],
                      'baseline_ms':baseline_ms,'candidate_ms':candidate_ms}))

if __name__=='__main__':main()

ARQUIVO: nexus_m/autosynth_cli.py

"""CLI for bounded, LLM-free source synthesis and optimization experiments."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse,json
from pathlib import Path
from .alignment_and_value_core import AlignmentGuard,ResourcePolicy
from .search_driven_improver import SearchDrivenImprover
from .ast_program_synthesis import RouterSpec,compile_source
from .ast_formal_gate import verify
from .ast_performance_profiler import profile
from .capacity_planner import plan


def main(argv=None):
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description='NEXUS AutoSynth: MCTS AST synthesis without an LLM')
    sub=p.add_subparsers(dest='command',required=True)
    s=sub.add_parser('search',help='discover improved managed router with MCTS')
    s.add_argument('--output',default='runs/autosynth')
    s.add_argument('--simulations',type=int,default=96)
    s.add_argument('--seed',type=int,default=2026)
    s.add_argument('--min-gain',type=float,default=.05)
    s.add_argument('--approve',action='store_true',help='permit reviewed managed-plugin source promotion')
    prof=sub.add_parser('profile',help='profile bottlenecks in the managed router')
    prof.add_argument('--output',default='runs/autosynth')
    prof.add_argument('--iterations',type=int,default=100)
    cap=sub.add_parser('capacity',help='plan compute and sharding WITHOUT provisioning cloud')
    cap.add_argument('--agents',type=int,default=100)
    cap.add_argument('--width',type=int,default=10000)
    cap.add_argument('--nodes',type=int,default=100000)
    cap.add_argument('--simulations',type=int,default=100000)
    cap.add_argument('--workers',type=int,default=4)
    r=sub.add_parser('rollback',help='restore previous managed router')
    r.add_argument('--output',default='runs/autosynth')
    r.add_argument('--approve',action='store_true')
    v=sub.add_parser('verify',help='finite exhaustive verification + structural termination check')
    v.add_argument('--strategy',choices=('sort','heap','scan','reverse_sort'),default='heap')
    v.add_argument('--key',choices=('stable','score','index'),default='stable')
    v.add_argument('--validation',choices=('strict','omitted'),default='strict')
    args=p.parse_args(argv)
    if args.command=='capacity':
        print(json.dumps(plan(args.agents,args.width,args.nodes,
                              args.simulations,args.workers).as_dict(),indent=2));return 0
    if args.command=='verify':
        spec=RouterSpec(args.strategy,args.key,args.validation)
        print(json.dumps({'source':compile_source(spec),'evidence':verify(spec).as_dict()},
                         indent=2,ensure_ascii=False));return 0
    guard=AlignmentGuard(Path(args.output),ResourcePolicy())
    improver=SearchDrivenImprover(guard,min_gain=getattr(args,'min_gain',.05))
    if args.command=='profile':
        print(json.dumps(profile(improver,iterations=args.iterations).as_dict(),
                         ensure_ascii=False,indent=2));return 0
    if args.command=='search':
        report=improver.run(approve=args.approve,simulations=args.simulations,
                            seed=args.seed)
        print(json.dumps(report.as_dict(),ensure_ascii=False,indent=2));return 0
    ok=improver.rollback(approve=args.approve)
    print(json.dumps({'rolled_back':ok,'active':str(improver.active)}));return 0

if __name__=='__main__':raise SystemExit(main())

"""Reproducible, leakage-controlled benchmarks and architectural ablations."""
from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
import csv,json,random,time,statistics
import torch
from .tasks import sample_task
from .grammar import run_many,value_on_examples
from .mcts import SharedMCTS
from .model import NeuralSociety
from .learning import TrainSettings,train
from .memory import PersistentMemory
from .telemetry import Telemetry
from .precision import network_dtype


def eval_one(task,result)->dict:
    if result.program:
        predicted=run_many(result.program,[x for x,_ in task.heldout])
        correct=sum(int(p==y) for p,(_,y) in zip(predicted,task.heldout))
        accuracy=correct/max(1,len(task.heldout))
    else:accuracy=0.0
    return {'family':task.family,'example_exact':int(result.example_exact),
            'ood_accuracy':float(accuracy),'ood_exact':int(accuracy==1.),
            'simulations':result.simulations,'expanded_nodes':result.expanded_nodes,
            'max_depth_reached':result.max_depth_reached,'source':result.source,
            'elapsed_sec':result.elapsed_sec,
            'program':' '.join(result.program or ())}


def evaluate_configuration(model,memory,task_list,simulations=100,max_depth=15,
                           seed=2026,communication=True,neural=True,
                           use_memory=True,telemetry=None):
    rows=[]
    for i,task in enumerate(task_list):
        searcher=SharedMCTS(model=model if neural else None,
             memory=memory if use_memory else None,
             telemetry=telemetry,simulations=simulations,max_depth=max_depth,
             seed=seed+i*8191, use_neural=neural,
             ablate_communication=not communication)
        # Never persist OOD evaluation task solutions: no test-time memory leakage.
        result=searcher.solve(task.public(),store=False)
        rows.append(eval_one(task,result))
    return rows


def aggregate(rows):
    if not rows:return {}
    keys=('example_exact','ood_accuracy','ood_exact','simulations','expanded_nodes','elapsed_sec')
    return {k:float(statistics.mean(r[k] for r in rows)) for k in keys}


def run_benchmark(output_dir,widths=(100,1000,10000),seeds=(2026,),agents=100,
                  steps=1000,simulations=100,episodes=12,precision='adaptive',
                  message_dim=16,top_k=4,recurrence=2,
                  max_depth=15,run_ablation=True,telemetry_level='summary'):
    """Train each width and evaluate OOD tasks + controls.

    Large widths and 1000 steps can be CPU-expensive. Reporting actual timings
    rather than asserting runtimes or accuracy ahead of measurement.
    """
    output=Path(output_dir);output.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    all_rows=[];summary=[]
    for seed in seeds:
        rng=random.Random(seed+999)
        # OOD tasks are generated once per seed and reused at every width.
        tasks=[sample_task(rng,'ood',5,20) for _ in range(episodes)]
        for width in widths:
            run_dir=output/f'seed_{seed}'/f'width_{width}'
            run_dir.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
            torch.manual_seed(seed+width)
            model=NeuralSociety(agents=agents,width=width,message_dim=message_dim,
                               top_k=min(top_k,agents-1),recurrence=recurrence)
            model=model.to(dtype=network_dtype(precision))
            with PersistentMemory(run_dir/'memory.sqlite') as memory, Telemetry(
                     run_dir/'telemetry',level=telemetry_level,
                     checkpoint_every=0) as logs:
                log_every=max(1,steps//20)
                settings=TrainSettings(steps=steps,seed=seed,log_every=log_every,
                                       checkpoint_every=steps if steps else 0)
                training=train(model,settings,run_dir,memory,logs)
                model.eval()
                scenarios=[('neural_collective_memory',True,True,True)]
                if run_ablation:
                    scenarios.extend([
                       ('neural_collective_no_memory',True,True,False),
                       ('neural_isolated',False,True,False),
                       ('uniform_mcts',False,False,False)])
                for name,comms,neural,remember in scenarios:
                    rows=evaluate_configuration(model,memory,tasks,simulations,max_depth,
                         seed,comms,neural,remember,telemetry=None)
                    for i,row in enumerate(rows):
                        all_rows.append({'seed':seed,'width':width,'agents':agents,
                                         'scenario':name,'task_index':i,**row})
                    agg=aggregate(rows)
                    summary.append({'seed':seed,'width':width,'agents':agents,'scenario':name,
                                    'neurons':width*agents,'parameters':model.report()['trainable_parameters'],
                                    'train_sec':training['duration_sec'],**agg})
                (run_dir/'architecture.json').write_text(json.dumps(model.report(),indent=2))
            write_csv(output/'results_by_task.csv',all_rows)
            write_csv(output/'summary.csv',summary)
            (output/'summary.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    return summary


def write_csv(path,rows):
    if not rows:return
    with Path(path).open('w',newline='',encoding='utf-8') as f:
        writer=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(rows[0]))
        writer.writeheader();writer.writerows(rows)


def plot_summary(csv_file,output_file):
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use('Agg')
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    with open(csv_file,newline='',encoding='utf-8') as f: rows=list(csv.DictReader(f))
    values={}
    for row in rows:
        key=(int(row['width']),row['scenario'])
        values.setdefault(key,[]).append(float(row['ood_exact']))
    widths=sorted(set(k[0] for k in values))
    names=sorted(set(k[1] for k in values))
    fig,ax=plt.subplots(figsize=(10,5))
    for n,name in enumerate(names):
        x=[i+(n-(len(names)-1)/2)*0.17 for i in range(len(widths))]
        y=[statistics.mean(values.get((w,name),[0])) for w in widths]
        ax.bar(x,y,width=.16,label=name)
    ax.set_xticks(list(range(len(widths))),[str(w) for w in widths])
    ax.set_xlabel('Neurons per agent')
    ax.set_ylabel('Exact OOD generalization rate')
    ax.set_ylim(0,1)
    ax.set_title('NEXUS-M: out-of-family verified program synthesis')
    ax.legend(fontsize=8)
    fig.tight_layout()
    Path(output_file).parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    fig.savefig(output_file,dpi=160)
    plt.close(fig)

"""Declarative hardware planning for distributed AST search (NO cloud calls)."""
from dataclasses import dataclass,asdict
import math

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CapacityPlan:
    agents:int
    neurons_each:int
    tree_nodes:int
    simulations:int
    worker_count:int
    total_neurons:int
    activation_megabytes_fp32:float
    estimated_tree_megabytes:float
    simulations_per_worker:int
    cloud_provisioned:bool
    approval_required:bool
    def as_dict(self):return asdict(self)

def plan(agents:int,neurons_each:int,tree_nodes:int,
         simulations:int,workers:int=4)->CapacityPlan:
    if not (1<=agents<=1000 and 1<=neurons_each<=10000 and
            1<=tree_nodes<=10_000_000 and 1<=simulations<=1_000_000 and
            1<=workers<=64):
        raise ValueError('capacity request exceeds bounded planning envelope')
    total=agents*neurons_each
    return CapacityPlan(agents,neurons_each,tree_nodes,simulations,workers,
        total,round(total*4/1e6,3),round(tree_nodes*160/1e6,3),
        math.ceil(simulations/workers),False,True)


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nexus_m/causal_inference_engine.py
SHA256: e46a34dd41574500faa1833e0456dc3328422c336851c367f0f6c3429023bdaa

"""Finite structural causal models, do-interventions and graphical criteria.

Implements exact binary SCM enumeration, the backdoor/frontdoor adjustment
formulas for their supported graph patterns, and a small physics-regression
experiment. NOT a complete do-calculus engine or a universal physics simulator.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
from itertools import combinations,product
from typing import Callable
import networkx as nx
import numpy as np
from networkx.algorithms.d_separation import is_d_separator


class CausalError(ValueError):pass

@dataclass
class CausalEstimate:
    value:float
    method:str
    identifiable:bool
    assumptions:list[str]
    def as_dict(self):return vars(self)


class CausalGraph:
    def __init__(self,edges:list[tuple[str,str]],nodes:list[str]|None=None,
                 latent:set[str]|None=None):
        self.graph=nx.DiGraph()
        self.graph.add_nodes_from(nodes or [])
        self.graph.add_edges_from(edges)
        if not nx.is_directed_acyclic_graph(self.graph):raise CausalError('causal graph must be a DAG')
        self.latent=set(latent or ())
        if not self.latent<=set(self.graph.nodes):raise CausalError('unknown latent node')

    def backdoor_valid(self,x:str,y:str,z:set[str]|None=None)->bool:
        z=set(z or ())
        if not {x,y}|z<=set(self.graph.nodes):raise CausalError('unknown node')
        if any(a in nx.descendants(self.graph,x) for a in z):return False
        if z & self.latent:return False
        backdoor=self.graph.copy()
        backdoor.remove_edges_from(list(backdoor.out_edges(x)))
        return bool(is_d_separator(backdoor,{x},{y},z))

    def backdoor_sets(self,x:str,y:str,max_set=4):
        options=sorted(set(self.graph.nodes)-self.latent-{x,y}-nx.descendants(self.graph,x))
        sets=[]
        for length in range(min(len(options),max_set)+1):
            for subset in combinations(options,length):
                if self.backdoor_valid(x,y,set(subset)):sets.append(subset)
        return sets

    def frontdoor_valid(self,x:str,y:str,m:str)->bool:
        if len({x,y,m})<3:return False
        if self.latent.intersection({x,y,m}):return False
        if m not in self.graph:return False
        if not nx.has_path(self.graph,x,m) or not nx.has_path(self.graph,m,y):return False
        # Every directed path from X to Y passes through mediator M.
        if any(m not in path for path in nx.all_simple_paths(self.graph,x,y)):return False
        return self.backdoor_valid(x,m,set()) and self.backdoor_valid(m,y,{x})


class BinarySCM:
    """Small SCM: each binary node is a function(parents, exogenous_bit).

    All exogenous bits are mutually independent with configurable Bernoulli
    probabilities. Probabilities are calculated by exact enumeration; maximum
    of 16 endogenous variables avoids exponential blow-up.
    """
    def __init__(self,graph:CausalGraph,functions:dict[str,Callable],
                 exogenous_probabilities:dict[str,float]|None=None):
        self.graph=graph
        self.order=list(nx.topological_sort(graph.graph))
        if len(self.order)>16:raise CausalError('exact enumeration limited to 16 nodes')
        if set(functions)!=set(self.order):raise CausalError('one structural function per node')
        self.functions=functions
        self.noise_p={v:float((exogenous_probabilities or {}).get(v,0.5)) for v in self.order}
        if any(not 0<=v<=1 for v in self.noise_p.values()):raise CausalError('invalid noise probability')

    def _enumerate(self,do:dict[str,int]|None=None):
        do=do or {}
        if not set(do)<=set(self.order) or any(v not in (0,1) for v in do.values()):
            raise CausalError('invalid do intervention')
        for noise in product((0,1),repeat=len(self.order)):
            probability=1.
            states={}
            for name,bit in zip(self.order,noise):
                p=self.noise_p[name]
                probability*=p if bit else 1-p
                parents={parent:states[parent] for parent in self.graph.graph.predecessors(name)}
                states[name]=do[name] if name in do else int(self.functions[name](parents,bit))
                if states[name] not in (0,1):raise CausalError('structural functions must return binary')
            if probability>0:yield states,probability

    def probability(self,query:dict[str,int],condition:dict[str,int]|None=None,
                    do:dict[str,int]|None=None)->float:
        condition=condition or {}
        variables=set(query)|set(condition)
        if not variables<=set(self.order):raise CausalError('unknown variable')
        total=0.;success=0.
        for row,weight in self._enumerate(do):
            if all(row[k]==v for k,v in condition.items()):
                total+=weight
                if all(row[k]==v for k,v in query.items()):success+=weight
        if total<1e-12:raise CausalError('zero-probability conditioning event')
        return success/total

    def do_effect(self,x:str,y:str,value:int)->CausalEstimate:
        return CausalEstimate(self.probability({y:1},do={x:value}),
                              'exact_SCM_do_enumeration',True,
                              ['fully specified binary structural functions',
                               'independent exogenous noises'])

    def identify_backdoor(self,x:str,y:str,value:int,adjust_for:tuple[str,...]|None=None)->CausalEstimate:
        choices=self.graph.backdoor_sets(x,y)
        if adjust_for is None:
            if not choices:return CausalEstimate(float('nan'),'backdoor',False,['no observed valid adjustment set'])
            adjust_for=choices[0]
        if not self.graph.backdoor_valid(x,y,set(adjust_for)):
            return CausalEstimate(float('nan'),'backdoor',False,['invalid backdoor adjustment'])
        answer=0.
        for vals in product((0,1),repeat=len(adjust_for)):
            z=dict(zip(adjust_for,vals))
            p_z=self.probability(z)
            if not p_z:continue
            answer+=self.probability({y:1},{x:value,**z})*p_z
        return CausalEstimate(answer,'backdoor_adjustment',True,
                              ['causal DAG correct','no unblocked confounders after adjustment','positivity'])

    def identify_frontdoor(self,x:str,y:str,m:str,value:int)->CausalEstimate:
        if not self.graph.frontdoor_valid(x,y,m):
            return CausalEstimate(float('nan'),'frontdoor',False,['frontdoor graphical criterion not satisfied'])
        answer=0.
        for mediator in (0,1):
            p_m=self.probability({m:mediator},{x:value})
            if not p_m:continue
            sum_x=0.
            for observed_x in (0,1):
                p_x=self.probability({x:observed_x})
                if p_x:sum_x+=self.probability({y:1},{m:mediator,x:observed_x})*p_x
            answer+=p_m*sum_x
        return CausalEstimate(answer,'frontdoor_adjustment',True,
                              ['causal DAG correct','all frontdoor criteria hold','positivity'])


@dataclass
class PhysicsFit:
    coefficients:dict[str,float]
    heldout_r2:float
    family:str


def infer_linear_damped_motion(positions,velocities,accelerations,seed=2026)->PhysicsFit:
    """Fit a = c_x*x + c_v*v + c_0; one restricted physics family."""
    x=np.asarray(positions,dtype=float)
    v=np.asarray(velocities,dtype=float)
    a=np.asarray(accelerations,dtype=float)
    if len(x)!=len(v) or len(x)!=len(a) or len(x)<12:
        raise ValueError('at least 12 equal-length measurements')
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)) or not np.all(np.isfinite(v)) or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        raise ValueError('measurements must be finite')
    design=np.column_stack([x,v,np.ones(len(x))])
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    order=rng.permutation(len(x));split=max(8,int(.75*len(x)))
    train,test=order[:split],order[split:]
    coefs,_,_,_=np.linalg.lstsq(design[train],a[train],rcond=None)
    predicted=design[test]@coefs
    denominator=np.sum((a[test]-a[test].mean())**2)
    r2=1-float(np.sum((a[test]-predicted)**2))/denominator if denominator>1e-12 else float('nan')
    return PhysicsFit({'position_coefficient':float(coefs[0]),'velocity_coefficient':float(coefs[1]),
                       'intercept':float(coefs[2])},r2,'damped_linear_motion')
