"""Continual synthetic meta-training, search distillation and task-time adaptation.

All oracle actions are visible ONLY to the training pipeline. During search
and evaluation, the agent sees few-shot examples and no oracle/heldout labels.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
import json,random,time
import torch
from torch.nn import functional as F
from .model import NeuralSociety
from .tasks import Task,sample_task
from .features import encode
from .grammar import TOKEN_TO_ID,value_on_examples
from .mcts import SharedMCTS
from .memory import PersistentMemory
from .telemetry import Telemetry

@dataclass
class TrainSettings:
    steps:int=1000
    lr:float=0.0008
    seed:int=2026
    log_every:int=50
    checkpoint_every:int=200
    search_improve_every:int=0
    search_simulations:int=100
    example_count:int=5
    precision:str='float32'


def supervised_loss(model:NeuralSociety,task:Task,program=None,
                    adapt_states=False):
    program=tuple(program or task.oracle or ())
    if not program:raise ValueError('Expected known training program')
    parameter=next(model.parameters())
    # Random prefix selection avoids expensive unrolling of all tokens per step.
    t=random.randrange(len(program))
    features=encode(task.public(),program[:t],device=parameter.device,dtype=parameter.dtype)
    output=model(features)
    target=torch.tensor([TOKEN_TO_ID[program[t]]],device=parameter.device)
    policy_loss=F.cross_entropy(output.logits.unsqueeze(0),target)
    # Favor accurate value predictions at solved/unsolved states, not unchecked claims.
    target_value=torch.tensor(0.85 if t>=len(program)-2 else 0.45,
                              device=parameter.device,dtype=parameter.dtype)
    critic_loss=(output.value-target_value).square()
    # Soft penalty on collapsed influence distribution.
    entropy=-(output.influence*output.influence.clamp_min(1e-9).log()).sum()
    return policy_loss+0.15*critic_loss-0.003*entropy,output


def save_checkpoint(path,model,optimizer,step,config):
    path=Path(path);path.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    torch.save({'step':step,'architecture':model.report(),
                'state_dict':model.state_dict(),
                'optimizer':optimizer.state_dict() if optimizer else None,
                'training_config':config},path)


def restore_checkpoint(path,device='cpu',with_optimizer=False)->tuple[NeuralSociety,dict]:
    # User-supplied pickle checkpoints should never be loaded from untrusted sources.
    bundle=torch.load(path,map_location=device,weights_only=True)
    cfg=bundle['architecture']
    model=NeuralSociety(agents=cfg['agents'],width=cfg['neurons_per_agent'],
                        message_dim=cfg['message_dim'],top_k=cfg['top_k'],
                        recurrence=cfg['recurrent_rounds'],communicate=cfg['communicate'])
    dtype=next(iter(bundle['state_dict'].values())).dtype
    model=model.to(device=device,dtype=dtype)
    model.load_state_dict(bundle['state_dict'])
    return model,bundle


def train(model:NeuralSociety,settings:TrainSettings,output_dir,
          memory:PersistentMemory|None=None,telemetry:Telemetry|None=None,
          allow_search_distillation=True):
    rng=random.Random(settings.seed)
    random.seed(settings.seed)
    torch.manual_seed(settings.seed)
    output=Path(output_dir);output.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    optimizer=torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(),lr=settings.lr,weight_decay=0.0001)
    model.train()
    history=[]
    started=time.monotonic()
    for step in range(1,settings.steps+1):
        task=sample_task(rng,'train',settings.example_count)
        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        loss,out=supervised_loss(model,task)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(),1.0)
        if telemetry and (telemetry.level=="full" or step==1 or step%settings.log_every==0):
            telemetry.neural(step,out)
            telemetry.weights(step,model)
        optimizer.step()
        if memory and step%max(1,settings.log_every)==0:
            # Training examples only; explicit provenance, not test leakage.
            memory.remember(task.public(),task.oracle,'training_oracle')
        if allow_search_distillation and settings.search_improve_every and step%settings.search_improve_every==0:
            probe=sample_task(rng,'train',settings.example_count)
            searcher=SharedMCTS(model=model,memory=memory,simulations=settings.search_simulations,
                                max_depth=15,seed=settings.seed+step)
            model.eval()
            found=searcher.solve(probe.public(),store=True)
            model.train()
            if found.example_exact and found.program:
                optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                distill, _=supervised_loss(model,probe,found.program)
                distill.backward()
                torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(),1.0)
                optimizer.step()
                if telemetry:telemetry.log('search_distillation',step=step,program=list(found.program),
                                           source=found.source)
        if step==1 or step%settings.log_every==0 or step==settings.steps:
            record={'step':step,'loss':float(loss.detach()),'elapsed_sec':time.monotonic()-started}
            history.append(record)
            if telemetry:telemetry.log('training',**record)
        if settings.checkpoint_every and step%settings.checkpoint_every==0:
            save_checkpoint(output/f'checkpoint_{step:06d}.pt',model,optimizer,step,vars(settings))
    save_checkpoint(output/'model_final.pt',model,optimizer,settings.steps,vars(settings))
    (output/'training_history.json').write_text(json.dumps(history,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    return {'steps':settings.steps,'duration_sec':time.monotonic()-started,
            'last_loss':history[-1]['loss'],'checkpoint':str(output/'model_final.pt')}


def adapt_to_examples(model:NeuralSociety,task:Task,steps=5,lr=0.0001,
                      persist=False)->dict:
    """Test-time self-supervision on GIVEN examples via leave-one-out prediction.

    The small adaptation objective estimates y from x/y of other examples via
    the value head as a proxy for consistency, and uses valid prefix likelihood.
    It does NOT secretly use any heldout targets or an unknown oracle.
    It is experimental: supervised task-specific adapters could be better.
    """
    if steps<=0:return {'steps':0,'loss':None}
    # Adapt only agent-level gain and routing bias; save/restore by default.
    names=('agent_gain','agent_routing_bias')
    previous={k:p.detach().clone() for k,p in model.named_parameters() if k in names}
    original={p:p.requires_grad for p in model.parameters()}
    for name,p in model.named_parameters():p.requires_grad_(name in names)
    try:
        optimizer=torch.optim.SGD([p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad],lr=lr)
        p=next(model.parameters());loss_value=0.
        # View the examples as a compact pattern, regularize updates against
        # unadapted activations rather than pretending the target program is known.
        reference=model(encode(task.public(),device=p.device,dtype=p.dtype))
        ref=reference.states.detach()
        for _ in range(steps):
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            out=model(encode(task.public(),device=p.device,dtype=p.dtype))
            # Input-conditioned consistency regularization: deliberately modest.
            loss=(out.states-ref).square().mean() + 0.002*out.messages.square().mean()
            loss.backward();optimizer.step()
            loss_value=float(loss.detach())
        return {'steps':steps,'loss':loss_value,'persist':persist,
                'note':'Unsupervised light adapter calibration; not guaranteed to improve accuracy'}
    finally:
        if not persist:
            with torch.no_grad():
                for name,p in model.named_parameters():
                    if name in previous:p.copy_(previous[name])
        for p,flag in original.items():p.requires_grad_(flag)


def adapt_to_program(model:NeuralSociety,task:Task,program,steps=5,lr=0.0002,
                     persist=False)->dict:
    """Temporary test-time distillation of a discovered program into adapters.

    The teacher is a candidate from MCTS matching PUBLIC examples; there is
    no access to oracle or hidden labels. A spurious candidate may hurt.
    """
    from .grammar import value_on_examples
    exact=value_on_examples(program,task.public().examples)[1]
    if not exact:raise ValueError('Only programs passing known examples can be adapted')
    if steps<1:return {'steps':0,'loss':None,'exact_on_examples':True}
    chosen=('agent_gain','agent_routing_bias','agent_bias')
    previous={name:p.detach().clone() for name,p in model.named_parameters() if name in chosen}
    flags={p:p.requires_grad for p in model.parameters()}
    for name,p in model.named_parameters():p.requires_grad_(name in chosen)
    optimizer=torch.optim.Adam([p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad],lr=lr)
    parameter=next(model.parameters())
    history=[]
    try:
        for _ in range(steps):
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            losses=[]
            for i,token in enumerate(program):
                feature=encode(task.public(),program[:i],device=parameter.device,dtype=parameter.dtype)
                predicted=model(feature)
                target=torch.tensor([TOKEN_TO_ID[token]],device=parameter.device)
                losses.append(F.cross_entropy(predicted.logits.unsqueeze(0),target))
            loss=torch.stack(losses).mean()
            loss.backward()
            torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_((p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad),1.0)
            optimizer.step()
            history.append(float(loss.detach()))
        return {'steps':steps,'first_loss':history[0], 'last_loss':history[-1],
                'exact_on_examples':exact,'persist':persist,
                'warning':'Adapts toward one verified-on-examples candidate; might overfit'}
    finally:
        if not persist:
            with torch.no_grad():
                for name,p in model.named_parameters():
                    if name in previous:p.copy_(previous[name])
        for p,flag in flags.items():p.requires_grad_(flag)


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nexus_m/mcts.py
SHA256: 9c591e7e0b499773a260aae722028c94dfef5dca37da3891395e1350c6cf3237

"""Shared PUCT-style Monte Carlo Tree Search for typed postfix programs.

Search values = float64 Python statistics. Verifier = exact bounded integers.
Policy can be uniform (rigorous random-network control) or a trained society.
Deep *maximum* depth does not imply searching all nodes at that depth.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass,field
from typing import Callable,Sequence
import math,random,time
import torch
from .grammar import TOKENS,TOKEN_TO_ID,valid_actions,value_on_examples,HALT
from .features import encode
from .tasks import Task
from .model import NeuralSociety
from .memory import PersistentMemory
from .telemetry import Telemetry

@dataclass
class SearchNode:
    prefix:tuple[str,...]
    parent:'SearchNode|None'=None
    prior:float=1.0
    visits:int=0
    score_sum:float=0.
    children:dict[str,'SearchNode']=field(default_factory=dict)
    expanded:bool=False

    @property
    def q(self):return self.score_sum/self.visits if self.visits else 0.

@dataclass
class SearchResult:
    program:tuple[str,...]|None
    example_exact:bool
    score:float
    simulations:int
    expanded_nodes:int
    max_depth_reached:int
    source:str
    elapsed_sec:float

    def as_dict(self):
        return {'program':list(self.program) if self.program else None,
                'example_exact':self.example_exact,'score':self.score,
                'simulations':self.simulations,'expanded_nodes':self.expanded_nodes,
                'max_depth_reached':self.max_depth_reached,
                'source':self.source,'elapsed_sec':self.elapsed_sec}

class SharedMCTS:
    def __init__(self,model:NeuralSociety|None=None, memory:PersistentMemory|None=None,
                 telemetry:Telemetry|None=None, max_depth=15, simulations=300,
                 exploration=1.5, rollout_depth=15, seed=2026,
                 use_neural=True, stop_on_exact=True, record_every=0,
                 ablate_communication=False,world_model=None,world_weight=0.0):
        self.model=model
        self.memory=memory
        self.telemetry=telemetry
        self.max_depth=max_depth
        self.simulations=simulations
        self.exploration=exploration
        self.rollout_depth=rollout_depth
        self.rng=random.Random(seed)
        self.use_neural=use_neural and model is not None
        self.stop_on_exact=stop_on_exact
        self.record_every=record_every
        self.ablate_communication=ablate_communication
        self.world_model=world_model
        self.world_weight=world_weight
        self.eval_cache={}
        self.expanded_nodes=0
        self.max_depth_reached=0

    def _priors_and_value(self,task,prefix):
        key=tuple(prefix)
        if key in self.eval_cache:return self.eval_cache[key]
        actions=valid_actions(prefix,self.max_depth)
        if not actions:return {},0.
        if not self.use_neural:
            priors={a:1/len(actions) for a in actions}
            result=(priors,0.5)
        else:
            p=next(self.model.parameters())
            with torch.no_grad():
                out=self.model(encode(task.public(),prefix,device=p.device,dtype=p.dtype),
                               force_no_messages=self.ablate_communication)
                indices=torch.tensor([TOKEN_TO_ID[a] for a in actions],device=p.device)
                logits=out.logits[indices].float()
                probs=torch.softmax(logits,dim=0).cpu().tolist()
                result=({a:float(p) for a,p in zip(actions,probs)},float(out.value.item()))
                if self.telemetry and self.telemetry.level=='full':
                    self.telemetry.neural(self.expanded_nodes,out)
        # Optional learned model of symbolic transitions: favor states whose
        # effect the predictor understands. This is a heuristic, not a proof.
        if self.world_model is not None and self.world_weight>0:
            priors,critic=result
            import math
            penalties={a:math.exp(-min(50,self.world_weight*self.world_model.surprise(task,key,a)))
                       for a in priors}
            total=sum(priors[a]*penalties[a] for a in priors)
            if total>0:
                priors={a:priors[a]*penalties[a]/total for a in priors}
                result=(priors,critic)
        self.eval_cache[key]=result
        return result

    def _expand(self,node,task):
        if node.expanded:return
        priors,_=self._priors_and_value(task,node.prefix)
        node.children={a:SearchNode(prefix=node.prefix+(a,),parent=node,prior=float(prior))
                       for a,prior in priors.items()}
        node.expanded=True
        self.expanded_nodes+=1

    def _select(self,node):
        sqrt_parent=math.sqrt(max(1,node.visits))
        return max(node.children.values(),key=lambda child:
                   child.q+self.exploration*child.prior*sqrt_parent/(1+child.visits))

    def _rollout(self,task,prefix):
        p=list(prefix)
        for _ in range(max(0,min(self.rollout_depth,self.max_depth-len(p)))):
            if p and p[-1]==HALT: break
            available=valid_actions(p,self.max_depth)
            if not available:break
            # Rollouts use neural probabilities with 60% probability;
            # uniform sampling otherwise prevents complete policy lock-in.
            if self.use_neural and self.rng.random()<0.6:
                prior,_=self._priors_and_value(task,tuple(p))
                a=self.rng.choices(list(prior),weights=list(prior.values()),k=1)[0]
            else:
                # Prefer compiling complete programs to obtain feedback.
                if HALT in available and self.rng.random()<0.33:
                    a=HALT
                else:
                    a=self.rng.choice(available)
            p.append(a)
        return tuple(p) if p and p[-1]==HALT else None

    def solve(self,task:Task, store=True)->SearchResult:
        """Only task.examples are used for the search; heldout tests are ignored."""
        started=time.monotonic()
        public=task.public()
        self.eval_cache.clear();self.expanded_nodes=0;self.max_depth_reached=0
        best=None;best_score=-1.0;best_source='search'
        if self.memory is not None:
            for hit in self.memory.retrieve(public):
                if hit['score']>best_score:
                    best_score=hit['score'];best=tuple(hit['program']);best_source='memory'
                if hit['exact']:
                    result=SearchResult(best,True,best_score,0,0,len(best),'memory',time.monotonic()-started)
                    if self.telemetry:self.telemetry.log('memory_hit',family=task.family,score=best_score,program=list(best))
                    return result
        root=SearchNode(prefix=())
        self._expand(root,public)
        iterations=0
        for t in range(self.simulations):
            iterations=t+1
            node=root;trajectory=[root]
            while node.expanded and node.children and node.prefix[-1:]!=(HALT,):
                node=self._select(node)
                trajectory.append(node)
                if node.visits==0: break
            self.max_depth_reached=max(self.max_depth_reached,len(node.prefix))
            evaluated=None
            if node.prefix[-1:]==(HALT,):
                evaluated=node.prefix
            elif len(node.prefix)<self.max_depth:
                self._expand(node,public)
                evaluated=self._rollout(public,node.prefix)
            reward=0.0
            if evaluated is not None:
                score,exact=value_on_examples(evaluated,public.examples)
                # Mild description-length preference; does not affect exact verification.
                reward=score*(1.0-0.002*len(evaluated))
                if score>best_score or (score==best_score and (best is None or len(evaluated)<len(best))):
                    best_score=score;best=evaluated;best_source='search'
                if exact and self.stop_on_exact:
                    for n in trajectory:
                        n.visits+=1;n.score_sum+=reward
                    break
            if evaluated is None and self.use_neural:
                _,critic=self._priors_and_value(public,node.prefix)
                reward=critic*0.2
            for n in trajectory:
                n.visits+=1;n.score_sum+=float(reward)
            if self.telemetry and self.record_every and (t+1)%self.record_every==0:
                self.telemetry.log('mcts',simulations=t+1,nodes=self.expanded_nodes,
                                   best_score=best_score,best_program=list(best) if best else None,
                                   max_depth=self.max_depth_reached)
        exact=bool(best and value_on_examples(best,public.examples)[1])
        if exact and store and self.memory:self.memory.remember(public,best,'mcts')
        result=SearchResult(best,exact,max(best_score,0),iterations,self.expanded_nodes,
                            self.max_depth_reached,best_source,time.monotonic()-started)
        if self.telemetry:self.telemetry.log('search_complete',**result.as_dict(),family=task.family)
        return result

"""Persistent, indexed, verified, replayable experience memory (SQLite WAL).

'verified' means consistent with the examples witnessed, not a proof that the
candidate matches an unknown underlying rule. Source examples and hashes stay.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import json, sqlite3, hashlib, time
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Sequence
from .grammar import value_on_examples, pretty
from .tasks import Task

DDL='''
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS episodes (
 id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
 fingerprint TEXT NOT NULL,
 family TEXT NOT NULL,
 examples_json TEXT NOT NULL,
 program_json TEXT NOT NULL,
 examples_exact INTEGER NOT NULL,
 score REAL NOT NULL,
 origin TEXT NOT NULL,
 created_at REAL NOT NULL,
 UNIQUE(fingerprint,program_json)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_family ON episodes(family, score DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_fingerprint ON episodes(fingerprint);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS concepts (
 id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
 canonical_program TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
 program_json TEXT NOT NULL,
 success_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
 failure_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
 last_used REAL NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS observations (
 id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
 timestamp REAL NOT NULL,
 kind TEXT NOT NULL,
 payload_json TEXT NOT NULL
);
'''

class PersistentMemory:
    def __init__(self, path: str | Path):
        self.path=Path(path)
        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        self.connection=sqlite3.connect(str(self.path),timeout=30)
        self.connection.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
        self.connection.executescript(DDL)

    def close(self):
        self.connection.commit()
        self.connection.close()

    def __enter__(self): return self
    def __exit__(self,*args): self.close()

    def remember(self,task:Task,program:Sequence[str],origin='mcts')->dict:
        prog=list(program)
        score,exact=value_on_examples(prog,task.examples)
        self.connection.execute('''INSERT OR REPLACE INTO episodes
          (fingerprint,family,examples_json,program_json,examples_exact,score,origin,created_at)
          VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)''',
          (task.fingerprint,task.family,json.dumps(task.examples),json.dumps(prog),int(exact),score,origin,time.time()))
        if exact:
            canonical=pretty(prog)
            self.connection.execute('''INSERT INTO concepts
              (canonical_program,program_json,success_count,failure_count,last_used)
              VALUES (?,?,1,0,?) ON CONFLICT(canonical_program)
              DO UPDATE SET success_count=success_count+1,last_used=excluded.last_used''',
              (canonical,json.dumps(prog),time.time()))
        self.connection.commit()
        return {'exact_on_examples':exact,'score':score}

    def retrieve(self, task:Task,limit=12)->list[dict]:
        """Rank candidate programs by CURRENT evidence, never hidden test labels.

        Mix exact-fingerprint episodes and consolidated concepts. Inference
        receives an unlabeled task; no family name is used as a shortcut.
        """
        rows=self.connection.execute("""SELECT program_json,origin FROM episodes
              WHERE examples_exact=1 ORDER BY (fingerprint=?) DESC,id DESC LIMIT 350""",
              (task.fingerprint,)).fetchall()
        concepts=self.connection.execute("""SELECT program_json FROM concepts
              ORDER BY success_count DESC,last_used DESC LIMIT 350""").fetchall()
        rows += [(r[0],'consolidated_concept') for r in concepts]
        seen=set();candidates=[]
        for program_json,origin in rows:
            if program_json in seen:continue
            seen.add(program_json)
            candidate=json.loads(program_json)
            score,exact=value_on_examples(candidate,task.examples)
            candidates.append({'program':candidate,'score':score,'exact':exact,
                               'origin':origin,'family':'unlabeled'})
        # Simplicity is a prior; input-output matches can still be spurious.
        candidates.sort(key=lambda x:(x['exact'],x['score'],-len(x['program'])),reverse=True)
        return candidates[:limit]

    def concepts(self,limit=20)->list[dict]:
        rows=self.connection.execute('''SELECT canonical_program,success_count,failure_count
           FROM concepts ORDER BY success_count DESC,last_used DESC LIMIT ?''',(limit,)).fetchall()
        return [dict(zip(('expression','successes','failures'),row)) for row in rows]

    def stats(self)->dict:
        ep=self.connection.execute('SELECT count(*),sum(examples_exact) FROM episodes').fetchone()
        n=self.connection.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM concepts').fetchone()[0]
        return {'episodes':ep[0], 'example_verified_episodes':ep[1] or 0,'concepts':n}

    def observation(self,kind,payload):
        self.connection.execute('INSERT INTO observations (timestamp,kind,payload_json) VALUES (?,?,?)',
                                (time.time(),kind,json.dumps(payload,ensure_ascii=False,default=str)))
        self.connection.commit()

    def snapshot(self,destination:str|Path):
        with sqlite3.connect(str(destination)) as target:
            self.connection.backup(target)

SHA256: 336a114cffe0b189934a42361c2c3776e101838f336d1e47840dc71e146b54f2

"""Supervised search-distillation into NEXUS-M policies on verified examples.

Verified here means consistent with supplied examples, not formally verified
against every input or the unknown hidden rule. Updates are bounded and can
be kept episodic without altering a stored checkpoint.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass,asdict
import copy
import torch
from torch.nn import functional as F
from .model import NeuralSociety
from .features import encode
from .tasks import Task
from .grammar import TOKEN_TO_ID
from .hierarchical_abstraction_search import evaluate_macro_program

_MACRO_TOKEN={'ADD_1':'ADD','ADD_2':'ADD','ADD_3':'ADD',
              'SUB_1':'SUB','SUB_2':'SUB','MUL_2':'MUL','MUL_3':'MUL',
              'NEG':'NEG','ABS':'ABS','SQUARE':'SQUARE','HALF_EVEN':'HALF'}

@dataclass
class DistillationResult:
    initial_loss:float
    final_loss:float
    steps:int
    persisted:bool
    sample_consistent:bool
    def as_dict(self):return asdict(self)


def distill_macro_program(model:NeuralSociety,examples:list[tuple[int,int]],
                          program:list[str],steps:int=20,lr:float=.0008,
                          persist:bool=True):
    if not 1<=steps<=100:raise ValueError('max 100 temporary update steps')
    if not 0<lr<=.01:raise ValueError('learning rate out of range')
    if not program or any(op not in _MACRO_TOKEN for op in program):
        raise ValueError('program uses unknown macro')
    if evaluate_macro_program(program,[x for x,_ in examples])!=[y for _,y in examples]:
        raise ValueError('cannot distill a program inconsistent with evidence')
    work=model if persist else copy.deepcopy(model)
    task=Task('unlabeled',tuple(examples),(),None)
    parameter=next(work.parameters())
    optimizer=torch.optim.AdamW(work.parameters(),lr=lr)
    targets=[_MACRO_TOKEN[name] for name in program]
    original_mode=work.training
    work.train(); losses=[]
    for step in range(steps):
        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        losses_each=[]
        for i,token in enumerate(targets):
            state=encode(task.public(),targets[:i],device=parameter.device,dtype=parameter.dtype)
            logits=work(state).logits
            label=torch.tensor([TOKEN_TO_ID[token]],device=parameter.device)
            losses_each.append(F.cross_entropy(logits.unsqueeze(0),label))
        loss=torch.stack(losses_each).mean()
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(work.parameters(),1.)
        optimizer.step()
        losses.append(float(loss.detach().item()))
    work.train(original_mode)
    return DistillationResult(losses[0],losses[-1],steps,persist,True)

ARQUIVO: nexus_m/model.py
SHA256: 62f10a30d8ebabfc222f34f2a654c53a12ab27a685addc1e943ddbbecdeb25b4

"""100-agent scalable recurrent neural society with learnable sparse messaging.

Width = actual activation count PER agent; parameters use factorized/shared
projections plus agent-specific adapters (not N dense HxH matrices).
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
import math
import torch
from torch import nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from .features import FEATURE_DIM
from .grammar import TOKENS

@dataclass
class CollectiveOutput:
    logits: torch.Tensor
    value: torch.Tensor
    states: torch.Tensor
    messages: torch.Tensor
    routing: torch.Tensor
    influence: torch.Tensor


class NeuralSociety(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, agents=100, width=100, message_dim=16, top_k=4,
                 recurrence=2, communicate=True):
        super().__init__()
        if agents<2 or width<4 or message_dim<2 or not 1<=top_k<agents:
            raise ValueError('Bad architecture dimensions')
        self.agents=agents
        self.width=width
        self.message_dim=message_dim
        self.top_k=top_k
        self.recurrence=recurrence
        self.communicate=communicate
        # Agent-specific weights and biases; O(agents*width), not O(agents*width²).
        self.agent_bias=nn.Parameter(torch.randn(agents,width)*0.025)
        self.agent_gain=nn.Parameter(torch.ones(agents,width)+torch.randn(agents,width)*0.01)
        self.agent_recur=nn.Parameter(torch.full((agents,width),0.20))
        self.agent_routing_bias=nn.Parameter(torch.randn(agents,message_dim)*0.05)
        self.encoder=nn.Linear(FEATURE_DIM,width)
        self.message_encoder=nn.Linear(width,message_dim)
        self.message_decoder=nn.Linear(message_dim,width,bias=False)
        self.query_proj=nn.Linear(message_dim,message_dim,bias=False)
        self.key_proj=nn.Linear(message_dim,message_dim,bias=False)
        self.action_head=nn.Linear(width,len(TOKENS))
        self.value_head=nn.Linear(width,1)
        self.influence_head=nn.Linear(width,1)
        self.halt_gate=nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(-0.5))

    def forward(self, features: torch.Tensor, prior_states=None,
                force_no_messages=False, diagnostics=False) -> CollectiveOutput:
        if features.ndim!=1 or features.shape[0]!=FEATURE_DIM:
            raise ValueError(f'Expected ({FEATURE_DIM},) input')
        base=torch.tanh(self.encoder(features))[None,:]
        states=(torch.zeros_like(self.agent_bias) if prior_states is None else prior_states)
        last_routing=None
        last_msg=None
        for _ in range(max(1,self.recurrence)):
            normalized=F.layer_norm(states,(self.width,))
            # Independent per-agent hidden state with trainable adapters.
            candidate=torch.tanh(base*self.agent_gain+self.agent_bias +
                                 self.agent_recur*normalized)
            msg=torch.tanh(self.message_encoder(candidate)+self.agent_routing_bias)
            if self.communicate and not force_no_messages:
                q=F.normalize(self.query_proj(msg),dim=-1,eps=1e-6)
                k=F.normalize(self.key_proj(msg),dim=-1,eps=1e-6)
                scores=(q @ k.T)/math.sqrt(self.message_dim)
                scores=scores.masked_fill(torch.eye(self.agents,dtype=torch.bool,device=scores.device),-1e6)
                chosen=scores.topk(self.top_k,dim=-1)
                sparse_w=torch.softmax(chosen.values,dim=-1)
                incoming=(sparse_w.unsqueeze(-1)*msg[chosen.indices]).sum(dim=1)
                # Non-selected links are exactly zero; top-k routing is data dependent.
                last_routing=torch.zeros_like(scores).scatter(1,chosen.indices,sparse_w)
                states=torch.tanh(candidate+0.65*self.message_decoder(incoming))
            else:
                last_routing=torch.zeros((self.agents,self.agents),device=msg.device,dtype=msg.dtype)
                states=candidate
            last_msg=msg
        importance=torch.softmax(self.influence_head(states).squeeze(-1),dim=0)
        logits=(importance.unsqueeze(-1)*self.action_head(states)).sum(dim=0)
        value=torch.sigmoid((importance.unsqueeze(-1)*self.value_head(states)).sum())
        return CollectiveOutput(logits,value,states,last_msg,last_routing,importance)

    @property
    def neuron_count(self): return self.agents*self.width

    def report(self):
        return {'agents':self.agents,'neurons_per_agent':self.width,
                'total_neurons':self.neuron_count,
                'trainable_parameters':sum(p.numel() for p in self.parameters()),
                'top_k':self.top_k,'message_dim':self.message_dim,
                'recurrent_rounds':self.recurrence,'communicate':self.communicate}

ARQUIVO: nexus_m/precision.py

"""Adaptive numeric precision: FP32/FP64 nets, FP64 search, exact DSL,
Decimal arbitrary-precision sandbox for fractional calculations.

Extra decimal digits never imply additional learned information.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from decimal import Decimal, localcontext
import torch


def network_dtype(precision: str):
    if precision in ('float32','adaptive'):return torch.float32
    if precision=='float64': return torch.float64
    raise ValueError('Choose float32, float64 or adaptive; arbitrary precision is in exact verifier')


def high_precision_decimal(value: str, digits=80) -> str:
    if not 16<=digits<=1000: raise ValueError('digits must be 16..1000')
    with localcontext() as ctx:
        ctx.prec=digits
        return format(Decimal(value)*Decimal(1), 'f')


def decimal_matmul_2x2(a,b,digits=80):
    """Small illustrative exact-decimal numeric kernel, NOT a PyTorch trainable layer."""
    if len(a)!=2 or len(b)!=2 or any(len(r)!=2 for r in a+b):
        raise ValueError('Expected two 2x2 matrices')
    with localcontext() as ctx:
        ctx.prec=digits
        aa=[[Decimal(str(v)) for v in row] for row in a]
        bb=[[Decimal(str(v)) for v in row] for row in b]
        return [[str(sum(aa[i][k]*bb[k][j] for k in range(2))) for j in range(2)] for i in range(2)]


def describe_precision(mode):
    return {'float32':'~7 significant decimal digits in neural tensors',
            'float64':'~16 significant decimal digits in neural tensors',
            'adaptive':'neural FP32 + search statistics FP64 + exact integer DSL + optional Decimal' }[mode]

ARQUIVO: nexus_m/recursive_self_improvement.py
SHA256: e2dcf79512ae89ad19b47a9abbc64ed13aac9c45fcaf3b10f2fa3871f8e74c69
====================================================================================================
"""Audited, bounded self-optimization of ONE explicitly managed routing plugin.

The system benchmarks two reviewed algorithm templates, checks equivalence,
prepares a source patch and optionally promotes it after authorization.
No LLM code generation, unrestricted repository editing, unbounded autonomous
loops, self-modification of policy guards, unsupervised cloud provisioning or
claim of permanent/infinite intelligence growth.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass,asdict
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib,importlib.util,json,random,statistics,time
from .alignment_and_value_core import AlignmentGuard

SORT_TEMPLATE='''"""Managed router: deterministic top-k (sorting)."""\ndef route(scores, k):\n    if not 0 <= k <= len(scores):\n        raise ValueError("invalid k")\n    return sorted(range(len(scores)), key=lambda i: (-scores[i], i))[:k]\n'''

HEAP_TEMPLATE='''"""Managed router: deterministic top-k (heap)."""\nimport heapq\ndef route(scores, k):\n    if not 0 <= k <= len(scores):\n        raise ValueError("invalid k")\n    return heapq.nsmallest(k, range(len(scores)), key=lambda i: (-scores[i], i))\n'''

@dataclass
class ImprovementReport:
    proposed:str
    baseline_median_ms:float
    candidate_median_ms:float
    equivalent:bool
    relative_gain:float
    promoted:bool
    reason:str
    source_path:str
    def as_dict(self):return asdict(self)

class ManagedSelfImprover:
    def __init__(self,guard:AlignmentGuard,min_speedup:float=0.05):
        self.guard=guard
        self.managed=self.guard.workspace/'managed'
        self.managed.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        self.path=self.managed/'router.py'
        if not self.path.exists():
            self.path.write_text(SORT_TEMPLATE,encoding='utf-8')
        self.min_speedup=min_speedup
        self.rounds=0
        self._route=self._load(self.path)

    @staticmethod
    def _load(path:Path):
        # Only load locally generated and allowlisted templates, never arbitrary user code.
        source=path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        if source not in (SORT_TEMPLATE,HEAP_TEMPLATE):
            raise RuntimeError('managed source does not match reviewed allowlisted templates')
        spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('nexus_reviewed_router',str(path))
        module=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
        spec.loader.exec_module(module)
        return module.route

    def route(self,scores:list[float],k:int):return self._route(scores,k)

    @staticmethod
    def _benchmark(fn,batches):
        trials=[]
        for _ in range(3):
            start=time.perf_counter()
            for scores,k in batches:fn(scores,k)
            trials.append((time.perf_counter()-start)*1000)
        return statistics.median(trials)

    def step(self,approve:bool=False,seed:int=2026,batches:int=160)->ImprovementReport:
        self.rounds+=1
        if self.rounds>5:raise RuntimeError('maximum of five improvement attempts per process')
        active=self.path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        candidate=HEAP_TEMPLATE if active==SORT_TEMPLATE else SORT_TEMPLATE
        rng=random.Random(seed)
        examples=[([rng.uniform(-5,5) for _ in range(rng.choice((32,100,1000)))],4)
                  for _ in range(max(10,min(400,batches)))]
        # Explicit edge-cases, ties, finite values, degenerate k.
        correctness=[([1.,1.,0.],2),([-4.,-5.,-3.],1),([0.],0),([0.],1)]
        temp=self.managed/'candidate_router.py'
        temp.write_text(candidate,encoding='utf-8')
        try:
            proposed=self._load(temp)
            equivalent=all(self._route(values,k)==proposed(values,k)
                           for values,k in examples+correctness)
            baseline=self._benchmark(self._route,examples)
            alt=self._benchmark(proposed,examples)
            gain=(baseline-alt)/max(baseline,1e-12)
            promotion=False;reason='candidate failed equivalence tests'
            if equivalent:
                if gain<self.min_speedup:
                    reason=f'gain {gain:.3f} < required {self.min_speedup:.3f}'
                elif not approve:
                    reason='candidate is eligible but manual promotion not approved'
                else:
                    rel='managed/router.py'
                    if not self.guard.authorize('source_edit',{'relative_path':rel},approved=approve).allowed:
                        reason='source edit rejected by policy'
                    elif not self.guard.authorize('hot_swap',{'relative_path':rel},approved=approve).allowed:
                        reason='hot swap rejected by policy'
                    else:
                        backup=self.path.with_suffix('.previous.py')
                        backup.write_text(active,encoding='utf-8')
                        replacement=self.managed/'new_router.py'
                        replacement.write_text(candidate,encoding='utf-8')
                        try:
                            replacement.replace(self.path) # atomic within filesystem
                            self._route=self._load(self.path) # runtime function swap
                            assert all(self.route(values,k)==self._load(backup)(values,k)
                                       for values,k in correctness)
                            promotion=True;reason='passed equivalence, benchmark and promotion policy'
                        except Exception:
                            self.path.write_text(active,encoding='utf-8')
                            self._route=self._load(self.path)
                            raise
            report=ImprovementReport('heap' if candidate==HEAP_TEMPLATE else 'sort',
                         baseline,alt,equivalent,gain,promotion,reason,str(self.path))
            (self.managed/'improvement_reports.jsonl').open('a',encoding='utf-8').write(
                json.dumps(report.as_dict(),ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
            return report
        finally:
            temp.unlink(missing_ok=True)

    def optimize(self,rounds:int=1,approve:bool=False)->list[ImprovementReport]:
        if not 1<=rounds<=5:raise ValueError('bounded 1..5 improvement rounds only')
        return [self.step(approve=approve,seed=2026+i) for i in range(rounds)]

ARQUIVO: nexus_m/search_driven_improver.py
SHA256: 90931c310c69b72a8ec754d362ae28d6f355daf02c62e4287bce305727e88c78

"""No-LLM source optimization: MCTS -> AST -> tests -> staging -> approved swap.

Self-modification is restricted to a managed router plugin. Core safety/policy
files, arbitrary Python and network/cloud APIs are not candidate targets.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass,asdict
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib,heapq,json,os,statistics,time
from .ast_program_synthesis import RouterSpec,compile_source,validate_generated_source,reference_route
from .ast_mcts import ASTSynthesisMCTS
from .ast_candidate_runtime import evaluate
from .ast_formal_gate import verify,promotion_eligible
from .alignment_and_value_core import AlignmentGuard

@dataclass
class PromotionReport:
    selected:dict
    search_simulations:int
    explored_programs:int
    search_elapsed_s:float
    measured_train_gain:float
    heldout_gain:float
    heldout_equivalent:bool
    finite_cases:int
    structurally_terminating:bool
    universal_formal_proof:bool
    staged_source:str
    active_source:str
    active_before_sha256:str
    candidate_sha256:str
    promoted:bool
    reason:str
    audit_ledger_verified:bool
    def as_dict(self):return asdict(self)


def _digest(source:str)->str:return hashlib.sha256(source.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()

class SearchDrivenImprover:
    def __init__(self,guard:AlignmentGuard,min_gain:float=.05):
        self.guard=guard
        if not 0<=min_gain<1:raise ValueError('gain must be in 0..1')
        self.min_gain=min_gain
        self.folder=guard.workspace/'managed'
        self.folder.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        self.active=self.folder/'ast_router.py'
        if not self.active.exists():self.active.write_text(compile_source(RouterSpec()),encoding='utf-8')
        self.staged=self.folder/'candidate_ast_router.py'
        self._route=self._load(self.active.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))

    @staticmethod
    def _load(source:str):
        # Executed code MUST match one of the 24 canonical ASTs generated by
        # the in-house compiler. This is not a general arbitrary-code loader.
        validate_generated_source(source)
        builtins={'len':len,'range':range,'sorted':sorted,'min':min,
                  'list':list,'ValueError':ValueError}
        context={'__builtins__':builtins,'heapq':heapq}
        exec(compile(source,'<reviewed_generated_ast>','exec'),context)
        return context['route']

    def route(self,scores,k):return self._route(scores,k)

    def run(self,*,approve:bool=False,simulations:int=96,seed:int=2026,
            min_gain:float|None=None)->PromotionReport:
        if not self.guard.authorize('mcts',{'simulations':simulations,'depth':3}).allowed:
            raise ValueError('search exceeds explicit policy budget')
        if min_gain is not None and not 0<=min_gain<1:raise ValueError('bad threshold')
        threshold=self.min_gain if min_gain is None else min_gain
        previous=self.active.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        baseline_spec=validate_generated_source(previous)
        search=ASTSynthesisMCTS(simulations=simulations,seed=seed,
                                 baseline_spec=baseline_spec).search()
        candidate_spec=RouterSpec(**search.best_spec)
        source=search.best_source
        self.staged.write_text(source,encoding='utf-8')
        proof=verify(candidate_spec,max_n=5)
        heldout=evaluate(candidate_spec,seed=seed+999_991,large=True,
                         checks=180,timing=65,repetitions=5,timeout=14.,
                         baseline_spec=baseline_spec)
        reason='';promoted=False
        if not promotion_eligible(proof):
            reason='candidate did not pass finite-domain and structural gate'
        elif not heldout.equivalent:
            reason='held-out differential test failed'
        elif heldout.improvement<threshold:
            reason=f'held-out gain {heldout.improvement:.3f} < required {threshold:.3f}'
        elif not approve:
            reason='staged for inspection; human approval required for persistent change'
        elif not self.guard.authorize('source_edit',{'relative_path':'managed/ast_router.py'},approved=True).allowed:
            reason='policy rejected source modification'
        elif not self.guard.authorize('hot_swap',{'relative_path':'managed/ast_router.py'},approved=True).allowed:
            reason='policy rejected hot swap'
        else:
            backup=self.folder/'ast_router.previous.py'
            backup.write_text(previous,encoding='utf-8')
            replacement=self.folder/'ast_router.next.py'
            replacement.write_text(source,encoding='utf-8')
            try:
                new_fn=self._load(source)
                # Additional independent smoke cases before mutating live object.
                for vals in ([1.,1.,2.],[-10.,0.,10.],[],[0.],list(range(120))):
                    for k in (0,min(1,len(vals)),len(vals)):
                        if new_fn(vals,k)!=reference_route(vals,k):
                            raise AssertionError('hot swap smoke equivalence failed')
                os.replace(replacement,self.active)
                self._route=self._load(self.active.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
                promoted=True
                reason='approved promotion applied atomically; previous source backed up'
            except Exception:
                self.active.write_text(previous,encoding='utf-8')
                self._route=self._load(previous)
                raise
            finally: replacement.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        report=PromotionReport(candidate_spec.as_dict(),simulations,
               search.unique_candidates,search.elapsed_seconds,search.best_train_gain,
               heldout.improvement,heldout.equivalent,proof.checked_cases,
               proof.bounded_loops,proof.machine_checked_universal_proof,
               str(self.staged),str(self.active),_digest(previous),_digest(source),promoted,reason,
               self.guard.verify_ledger())
        reports=self.folder/'autosynth_reports.jsonl'
        with reports.open('a',encoding='utf-8') as f:
            f.write(json.dumps(report.as_dict(),ensure_ascii=False,sort_keys=True)+'\n')
        (self.folder/'ast_search_trace.json').write_text(
            json.dumps(search.as_dict(),indent=2,ensure_ascii=False),encoding='utf-8')
        (self.folder/'proof_evidence.json').write_text(
            json.dumps(proof.as_dict(),indent=2,ensure_ascii=False),encoding='utf-8')
        (self.folder/'heldout_measurement.json').write_text(
            json.dumps(heldout.as_dict(),indent=2,ensure_ascii=False),encoding='utf-8')
        return report

    def rollback(self,*,approve:bool=False)->bool:
        backup=self.folder/'ast_router.previous.py'
        if not backup.exists():return False
        if not approve:raise PermissionError('manual approval required for rollback')
        if not self.guard.authorize('hot_swap',{'relative_path':'managed/ast_router.py'},approved=True).allowed:
            raise PermissionError('rollback denied by policy')
        recovered=backup.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        fn=self._load(recovered)
        temp=self.folder/'ast_router.rollback.py'
        temp.write_text(recovered,encoding='utf-8')
        os.replace(temp,self.active)
        self._route=fn
        return True

"""Synthetic family-disjoint algorithmic tasks; no real-world data needed."""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
import hashlib, json, random
from .grammar import execute

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Task:
    family: str
    examples: tuple[tuple[int,int], ...]
    heldout: tuple[tuple[int,int], ...]
    oracle: tuple[str, ...] | None = None

    @property
    def fingerprint(self) -> str:
        data=json.dumps({"family":self.family,"examples":self.examples},sort_keys=True)
        return hashlib.sha256(data.encode()).hexdigest()[:24]

    def public(self) -> "Task":
        """Drop hidden labels and oracle before passing to a solver."""
        return Task("unlabeled", self.examples, (), None)


def generate_task(rng:random.Random, family:str, n_examples=5, n_test=20) -> Task:
    a=rng.choice((-3,-2,-1,1,2,3))
    b=rng.choice((-3,-2,-1,0,1,2,3))
    c=rng.choice((-3,-2,-1,1,2,3))
    x=['X']; ca=[f'C{a}']; cb=[f'C{b}']; cc=[f'C{c}']
    templates={
       'linear': x+ca+['MUL']+cb+['ADD'],
       'shift': x+ca+['ADD'],
       'square': x+['SQUARE']+cb+['ADD'],
       'absolute': x+['ABS']+cb+['ADD'],
       'parity': x+['MOD2']+ca+['ADD'],
       'negate': x+['NEG']+cb+['ADD'],
       'halved': x+['HALF']+cb+['ADD'],
       'shift_absolute': x+ca+['ADD','ABS']+cb+['ADD'],
       'quadratic': x+['SQUARE']+x+ca+['MUL','ADD']+cb+['ADD'],
       'parity_scaled': x+['MOD2']+ca+['MUL']+cb+['ADD'],
       'product_shift': x+ca+['ADD']+x+cc+['ADD','MUL'],
       'abs_square': x+['SQUARE']+ca+['SUB','ABS'],
    }
    if family not in templates: raise ValueError(f'Unknown family {family}')
    program=tuple(templates[family]+['HALT'])
    # The example and evaluation domains differ to measure extrapolation.
    candidates=list(range(-9,10))
    rng.shuffle(candidates)
    xs=candidates[:min(n_examples,len(candidates))]
    test_x=list(range(-35,-10))+list(range(11,36))
    rng.shuffle(test_x)
    test_x=test_x[:n_test]
    return Task(family,tuple((v,execute(program,v)) for v in xs),
                tuple((v,execute(program,v)) for v in test_x), program)

TRAIN_FAMILIES=('shift','linear','square','absolute','parity','negate','halved')
OOD_FAMILIES=('shift_absolute','quadratic','parity_scaled','product_shift','abs_square')


def sample_task(rng:random.Random, split='train', n_examples=5, n_test=20)->Task:
    families=TRAIN_FAMILIES if split=='train' else OOD_FAMILIES
    return generate_task(rng,rng.choice(families),n_examples,n_test)

SHA256: bbc8cfba8e9aedecc8847b9c019a3bc682ab87ae5d906f2124fd6ef924849874
====================================================================================================
"""Streaming event log, parameter-delta statistics and optional full tensor dumps.

'full' is genuinely expensive: O(agents*width*recorded_steps) activation
storage, plus NxN routing matrices and optionally all parameter snapshots.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import json, time, os
import torch

class Telemetry:
    def __init__(self, directory, level='summary', flush_every=1,
                 checkpoint_every=0, record_weight_tensors=False):
        if level not in ('off','summary','full'): raise ValueError('level=off|summary|full')
        self.directory=Path(directory)
        self.directory.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        (self.directory/'tensor_snapshots').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        self.level=level
        self.flush_every=max(1,flush_every)
        self.checkpoint_every=checkpoint_every
        self.record_weight_tensors=record_weight_tensors
        self.stream=None if level=='off' else (self.directory/'events.jsonl').open('a',encoding='utf-8',buffering=1)
        self.counter=0
        self.weights_previous={}

    def log(self,kind,**attributes):
        if self.stream is None:return
        self.counter+=1
        event={'utc':datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),'type':kind,'event_id':self.counter,**attributes}
        self.stream.write(json.dumps(event,ensure_ascii=False,default=str)+'\n')
        if self.counter%self.flush_every==0:self.stream.flush()

    @torch.no_grad()
    def neural(self,step,output,full_every=1):
        if self.level=='off' or step%max(1,full_every)!=0:return
        message=output.messages.detach().cpu()
        routing=output.routing.detach().cpu()
        self.log('communication',step=step,round_count=1,
                 messages=float((routing>0).sum().item()),
                 mean_message_norm=round(float(message.norm(dim=-1).mean()),6),
                 max_message_abs=round(float(message.abs().max()),6),
                 mean_active_routes=round(float((routing>0).sum(dim=-1).float().mean()),4),
                 influence_entropy=float(-(output.influence*output.influence.clamp_min(1e-12).log()).sum()),
                 state_rms=float(output.states.square().mean().sqrt()))
        if self.level=='full':
            path=self.directory/'tensor_snapshots'/f'neural_{step:09d}.pt'
            torch.save({'states':output.states.detach().cpu(),
                        'messages':message,'routing':routing,
                        'influence':output.influence.detach().cpu()},path)
            self.log('neural_dump',step=step,path=str(path.relative_to(self.directory)))

    @torch.no_grad()
    def weights(self,step,model):
        if self.level=='off':return
        changes={}; grad_norms={}; total=0
        for name,p in model.named_parameters():
            current=p.detach()
            total+=p.numel()
            current_norm=float(current.float().norm())
            if name in self.weights_previous:
                difference=(current.cpu().float()-self.weights_previous[name])
                delta=float(difference.norm())
            else: delta=None
            changes[name]={'norm':round(current_norm,6),'delta':None if delta is None else round(delta,6)}
            if p.grad is not None: grad_norms[name]=round(float(p.grad.detach().float().norm()),6)
            self.weights_previous[name]=current.cpu().float().clone()
        self.log('parameters',step=step,total_parameters=total,stats=changes,grad_norms=grad_norms)
        if self.record_weight_tensors or (self.checkpoint_every and step%self.checkpoint_every==0):
            output=self.directory/'tensor_snapshots'/f'weights_{step:09d}.pt'
            torch.save({n:p.detach().cpu() for n,p in model.named_parameters()},output)
            self.log('weights_dump',step=step,path=str(output.relative_to(self.directory)))

    def close(self):
        if self.stream:self.stream.flush();self.stream.close();self.stream=None
    def __enter__(self):return self
    def __exit__(self,*args):self.close()


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nexus_m/universal_compute.py
SHA256: 8f51060fa9927ad25ccd75805f51f857e12e6d1cffa5678e94fc65bead5657f0
====================================================================================================
"""Budgeted Python-subset interpreter, optional compiler diagnostics, proof adapters.

The subset (integer variables, if, while, arithmetic) is computationally
universal IN PRINCIPLE; the implementation intentionally enforces fuel, integer
size, loop and AST limits. It is not a security boundary for hostile native
compilers or a universal bug-freedom proof. No eval/exec of submitted code.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import ast
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from pathlib import Path
import operator
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
from typing import Any, Callable


class BudgetExceeded(RuntimeError):
    pass

class ProgramRejected(ValueError):
    pass

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ExecutionBudget:
    fuel: int = 20_000
    max_bits: int = 4096
    max_ast_nodes: int = 800
    max_source_chars: int = 15_000
    deadline_seconds: float = 2.0

@dataclass
class ExecutionResult:
    result: int | bool | None
    steps: int
    elapsed_seconds: float
    completed: bool
    error: str | None = None

BIN_OPS = {
    ast.Add: operator.add, ast.Sub: operator.sub, ast.Mult: operator.mul,
    ast.FloorDiv: operator.floordiv, ast.Mod: operator.mod,
}
CMP_OPS = {ast.Eq:operator.eq,ast.NotEq:operator.ne,ast.Lt:operator.lt,
           ast.LtE:operator.le,ast.Gt:operator.gt,ast.GtE:operator.ge}


class _ReturnSignal(Exception):
    def __init__(self, value): self.value = value


class BudgetedPython:
    """Interpret a restricted Python function `def main(x): ...`.

    All integer computations are exact. Loops are legal, but fuel is bounded.
    No imports, files, attributes, subprocess, native calls, introspection,
    arbitrary Python objects, eval, exec or dynamic code compilation.
    """
    def __init__(self, budget: ExecutionBudget | None = None):
        self.budget = budget or ExecutionBudget()
        self.steps = 0
        self.start = 0.0

    def _tick(self):
        self.steps += 1
        if self.steps > self.budget.fuel:
            raise BudgetExceeded('instruction fuel exhausted')
        if self.steps % 64 == 0 and time.monotonic()-self.start > self.budget.deadline_seconds:
            raise BudgetExceeded('wall-clock budget exhausted')

    def _check(self, x: Any):
        if type(x) not in (int,bool): raise ProgramRejected('only integer/bool values')
        if type(x) is int and x.bit_length() > self.budget.max_bits:
            raise BudgetExceeded('integer bit budget exceeded')
        return x

    @staticmethod
    def _syntax(source: str, budget: ExecutionBudget) -> ast.FunctionDef:
        if len(source) > budget.max_source_chars: raise ProgramRejected('source too long')
        try: tree = ast.parse(source)
        except SyntaxError as ex: raise ProgramRejected(str(ex)) from ex
        if sum(1 for _ in ast.walk(tree)) > budget.max_ast_nodes:
            raise ProgramRejected('AST node limit')
        if len(tree.body)!=1 or not isinstance(tree.body[0],ast.FunctionDef):
            raise ProgramRejected('one function required: def main(x)')
        fn=tree.body[0]
        if fn.name!='main' or len(fn.args.args)!=1 or fn.args.args[0].arg!='x' or fn.decorator_list or fn.args.defaults or fn.args.kwonlyargs:
            raise ProgramRejected('signature must be def main(x)')
        allowed=(ast.Module,ast.FunctionDef,ast.arguments,ast.arg,ast.Assign,
                 ast.AugAssign,ast.If,ast.While,ast.Return,ast.Pass,ast.Name,ast.Load,ast.Store,ast.Constant,
                 ast.BinOp,ast.UnaryOp,ast.BoolOp,ast.Compare,ast.IfExp,
                 ast.Add,ast.Sub,ast.Mult,ast.FloorDiv,ast.Mod,ast.UAdd,
                 ast.USub,ast.Not,ast.And,ast.Or,ast.Eq,ast.NotEq,ast.Lt,
                 ast.LtE,ast.Gt,ast.GtE)
        for node in ast.walk(fn):
            if not isinstance(node,allowed):
                raise ProgramRejected(f'unsupported construct: {type(node).__name__}')
            if isinstance(node,ast.Constant) and type(node.value) not in (int,bool):
                raise ProgramRejected('only integer/bool constants')
        return fn

    def _expr(self,node,env):
        self._tick()
        if isinstance(node,ast.Constant): return self._check(node.value)
        if isinstance(node,ast.Name):
            if node.id not in env: raise ProgramRejected(f'undefined variable {node.id}')
            return env[node.id]
        if isinstance(node,ast.BinOp):
            op=BIN_OPS.get(type(node.op))
            if op is None: raise ProgramRejected('unsupported arithmetic')
            a,b=self._expr(node.left,env),self._expr(node.right,env)
            if isinstance(node.op,(ast.FloorDiv,ast.Mod)) and b==0: raise ProgramRejected('division by zero')
            return self._check(op(a,b))
        if isinstance(node,ast.UnaryOp):
            val=self._expr(node.operand,env)
            if isinstance(node.op,ast.USub): return self._check(-val)
            if isinstance(node.op,ast.UAdd): return self._check(+val)
            if isinstance(node.op,ast.Not): return not val
        if isinstance(node,ast.IfExp):
            return self._expr(node.body if self._expr(node.test,env) else node.orelse,env)
        if isinstance(node,ast.BoolOp):
            if isinstance(node.op,ast.And):
                return all(self._expr(v,env) for v in node.values)
            if isinstance(node.op,ast.Or):
                return any(self._expr(v,env) for v in node.values)
        if isinstance(node,ast.Compare):
            left=self._expr(node.left,env)
            for op,right_node in zip(node.ops,node.comparators):
                right=self._expr(right_node,env)
                fn=CMP_OPS.get(type(op))
                if fn is None: raise ProgramRejected('unsupported comparison')
                if not fn(left,right): return False
                left=right
            return True
        raise ProgramRejected(f'bad expression: {type(node).__name__}')

    def _block(self,statements,env):
        for stmt in statements:
            self._tick()
            if isinstance(stmt,(ast.Assign,ast.AugAssign)):
                if isinstance(stmt,ast.Assign):
                    if len(stmt.targets)!=1 or not isinstance(stmt.targets[0],ast.Name):
                        raise ProgramRejected('assignment must target a variable')
                    env[stmt.targets[0].id]=self._expr(stmt.value,env)
                else:
                    if not isinstance(stmt.target,ast.Name):raise ProgramRejected('bad augmented assignment')
                    name=stmt.target.id
                    op=BIN_OPS.get(type(stmt.op))
                    if op is None or name not in env:raise ProgramRejected('bad augmented assignment')
                    rhs=self._expr(stmt.value,env)
                    if isinstance(stmt.op,(ast.Mod,ast.FloorDiv)) and rhs==0: raise ProgramRejected('division by zero')
                    env[name]=self._check(op(env[name],rhs))
            elif isinstance(stmt,ast.Return):
                raise _ReturnSignal(self._expr(stmt.value,env) if stmt.value else None)
            elif isinstance(stmt,ast.If):
                self._block(stmt.body if self._expr(stmt.test,env) else stmt.orelse,env)
            elif isinstance(stmt,ast.While):
                while self._expr(stmt.test,env):
                    self._tick()
                    self._block(stmt.body,env)
                if stmt.orelse: self._block(stmt.orelse,env)
            elif isinstance(stmt,ast.Pass):pass
            else: raise ProgramRejected(f'unsupported statement {type(stmt).__name__}')

    def run(self,source:str,x:int) -> ExecutionResult:
        self.steps=0;self.start=time.monotonic()
        try:
            fn=self._syntax(source,self.budget)
            env={'x':self._check(x)}
            try:
                self._block(fn.body,env)
                return ExecutionResult(None,self.steps,time.monotonic()-self.start,False,'missing return')
            except _ReturnSignal as signal:
                return ExecutionResult(signal.value,self.steps,time.monotonic()-self.start,True)
        except (ProgramRejected,BudgetExceeded,ZeroDivisionError,OverflowError) as ex:
            return ExecutionResult(None,self.steps,time.monotonic()-self.start,False,str(ex))


@dataclass
class ProofResult:
    status: str # proved, counterexample, bounded_pass, unknown
    method: str
    details: str
    checked_cases: int = 0
    witness: int | None = None


def bounded_equivalence(source_a:str,source_b:str,lo=-100,hi=100,
                        budget:ExecutionBudget|None=None)->ProofResult:
    """Exhaustive *finite-domain* verification, not general equivalence proof."""
    if hi<lo or hi-lo>100_000: raise ValueError('invalid finite domain')
    interpreter=BudgetedPython(budget)
    for i,x in enumerate(range(lo,hi+1),1):
        a=interpreter.run(source_a,x)
        b=interpreter.run(source_b,x)
        if not a.completed or not b.completed:
            return ProofResult('unknown','bounded_exhaustive','program failed or exceeded fuel',i,x)
        if a.result != b.result:
            return ProofResult('counterexample','bounded_exhaustive',f'outputs {a.result!r} and {b.result!r}',i,x)
    return ProofResult('bounded_pass','bounded_exhaustive',f'equivalent only for integers in [{lo},{hi}]',hi-lo+1)


def _parse_polynomial_expression(expression: str):
    """Translate only a tiny trusted AST to SymPy, never sympify arbitrary text."""
    import sympy as sp
    if len(expression)>300:raise ValueError('symbolic expression too long')
    tree=ast.parse(expression,mode='eval')
    if sum(1 for _ in ast.walk(tree))>60:raise ValueError('symbolic AST too large')
    x=sp.Symbol('x',integer=True)
    def parse(node):
        if isinstance(node,ast.Constant) and type(node.value) is int and abs(node.value)<10**9:
            return sp.Integer(node.value)
        if isinstance(node,ast.Name) and node.id=='x':return x
        if isinstance(node,ast.UnaryOp):
            val=parse(node.operand)
            if isinstance(node.op,ast.USub):return -val
            if isinstance(node.op,ast.UAdd):return val
        if isinstance(node,ast.BinOp):
            a,b=parse(node.left),parse(node.right)
            if isinstance(node.op,ast.Add):return a+b
            if isinstance(node.op,ast.Sub):return a-b
            if isinstance(node.op,ast.Mult):return a*b
            if isinstance(node.op,ast.Pow) and isinstance(node.right,ast.Constant):
                exponent=node.right.value
                if type(exponent) is int and 0<=exponent<=12:return a**exponent
        raise ValueError(f'unsupported symbolic expression: {type(node).__name__}')
    return parse(tree.body),x


def prove_polynomial_identity(left:str,right:str)->ProofResult:
    """Symbolic polynomial equivalence with strictly parsed exact expressions."""
    import sympy as sp
    try:
        a,x=_parse_polynomial_expression(left)
        b,_=_parse_polynomial_expression(right)
        if not a.is_polynomial(x) or not b.is_polynomial(x):
            return ProofResult('unknown','sympy-polynomial','non-polynomial expression')
        diff=sp.Poly(sp.cancel(a-b),x)
        if diff.is_zero:
            return ProofResult('proved','symbolic_polynomial','polynomial identity over integers')
        return ProofResult('counterexample','symbolic_polynomial',f'nonzero difference: {diff.as_expr()}')
    except (ValueError,TypeError,SyntaxError,sp.SympifyError) as ex:
        return ProofResult('unknown','sympy-polynomial',str(ex))


def optional_proof_assistant_check(assistant:str,proof_source:str,
                                    allow_external:bool=False,timeout:float=5.0)->dict:
    """Lean/Coq kernel adapter. It reports proof only when tool REALLY succeeds.

    Runs external theorem prover only on user-trusted source with opt-in. The
    host operating system must isolate untrusted source/processes separately.
    """
    if assistant not in ('lean','coq'):raise ValueError('assistant must be lean or coq')
    if not allow_external:
        return {'proved':False,'available':False,'reason':'external prover requires opt-in'}
    if not 0<len(proof_source)<=30_000:raise ValueError('invalid source size')
    tool='lean' if assistant=='lean' else 'coqc'
    executable=shutil.which(tool)
    if not executable:return {'proved':False,'available':False,'reason':f'{tool} not installed'}
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='nexus_proof_') as tmp:
        target=Path(tmp)/('Proof.lean' if assistant=='lean' else 'Proof.v')
        target.write_text(proof_source,encoding='utf-8')
        args=[executable,str(target)] if assistant=='lean' else [executable,'-q',str(target)]
        try:
            proc=subprocess.run(args,cwd=tmp,timeout=timeout,text=True,capture_output=True)
            return {'proved':proc.returncode==0,'available':True,'returncode':proc.returncode,
                    'diagnostics':(proc.stderr or proc.stdout)[-2000:]}
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            return {'proved':False,'available':True,'reason':'theorem prover timeout'}


COMPILER_COMMANDS={
    'python': None,
    'cpp': ('clang++','-std=c++17','-fsyntax-only','-x','c++'),
    'rust': ('rustc','--edition=2021','--emit=metadata'),
    'assembly': ('clang','-c','-x','assembler'),
}

def optional_compiler_check(language:str,source:str,allow_native_compiler:bool=False,
                            timeout:float=3.0)->dict:
    """Optional compilation diagnostics; compilation is NOT a formal proof.

    Native compilers process attacker-controlled text and are not a sandbox.
    This is OFF unless explicitly opted in on a trusted local machine.
    No compiled binary is executed.
    """
    if language not in COMPILER_COMMANDS:raise ValueError('language must be python/cpp/rust/assembly')
    if language=='python':
        try: ast.parse(source);return {'available':True,'syntax_ok':True,'proof':False}
        except SyntaxError as ex:return {'available':True,'syntax_ok':False,'error':str(ex),'proof':False}
    if not allow_native_compiler:
        return {'available':False,'reason':'native compiler opt-in required','proof':False}
    command=COMPILER_COMMANDS[language]
    executable=shutil.which(command[0])
    if not executable:return {'available':False,'reason':'compiler not installed','proof':False}
    if len(source)>100_000: raise ValueError('native source size exceeded')
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='nexus_compile_') as temp:
        ext={'cpp':'.cpp','rust':'.rs','assembly':'.s'}[language]
        target=Path(temp)/('candidate'+ext)
        target.write_text(source,encoding='utf-8')
        args=[executable,*command[1:],str(target)]
        if language=='rust':args.extend(['-o',str(Path(temp)/'candidate.rmeta')])
        if language=='assembly':args.extend(['-o',str(Path(temp)/'candidate.o')])
        try:
            cp=subprocess.run(args,cwd=temp,capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=timeout,check=False)
            return {'available':True,'syntax_ok':cp.returncode==0,'returncode':cp.returncode,
                    'diagnostics':(cp.stderr or cp.stdout)[-1500:],'proof':False}
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            return {'available':True,'syntax_ok':False,'reason':'compiler timeout','proof':False}


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nexus_m/world_model.py
SHA256: 4e674c8071255d65401d65e44d104b56c8819711d1dd43dc26dbd3ad74dd59e2
====================================================================================================
"""Optional learned predictive model of program-state transitions.

The *real* DSL simulator is exact; this auxiliary neural predictor learns how
state embeddings change when a token is appended. This is a limited world
model of a formal language, not a general model of the physical world.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
import json,random,time
import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F
from .features import encode,FEATURE_DIM
from .grammar import TOKENS,TOKEN_TO_ID
from .tasks import sample_task, Task

class HypothesisWorldModel(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self,hidden=96):
        super().__init__()
        self.hidden=hidden
        self.transition=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(FEATURE_DIM+len(TOKENS),hidden),
                      nn.GELU(),nn.Linear(hidden,hidden),nn.GELU(),
                      nn.Linear(hidden,FEATURE_DIM))

    def forward(self,features,action_ids):
        # Batch-compatible, including scalar feature/action pairs.
        if features.ndim==1:features=features.unsqueeze(0)
        ids=torch.as_tensor(action_ids,device=features.device,dtype=torch.long).reshape(-1)
        action_hot=F.one_hot(ids,len(TOKENS)).to(features.dtype)
        if len(action_hot)==1 and features.shape[0]>1:
            action_hot=action_hot.expand(features.shape[0],-1)
        return self.transition(torch.cat((features,action_hot),dim=-1))

    @torch.no_grad()
    def surprise(self,task:Task,prefix,action):
        device=next(self.parameters()).device
        before=encode(task.public(),prefix,device=device)
        after=encode(task.public(),tuple(prefix)+(action,),device=device)
        prediction=self(before,TOKEN_TO_ID[action]).squeeze(0)
        return float(F.mse_loss(prediction,after).item())


def train_world_model(output_dir,steps=1000,seed=2026,hidden=96,lr=0.001):
    """Learn predictive dynamics from supervised training-family transitions."""
    directory=Path(output_dir);directory.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    rng=random.Random(seed)
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    net=HypothesisWorldModel(hidden)
    optimizer=torch.optim.AdamW(net.parameters(),lr=lr)
    history=[];started=time.monotonic()
    for step in range(1,steps+1):
        task=sample_task(rng,'train')
        prefix=task.oracle[:rng.randrange(len(task.oracle))]
        action=task.oracle[len(prefix)]
        before=encode(task.public(),prefix)
        after=encode(task.public(),prefix+(action,))
        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        prediction=net(before,TOKEN_TO_ID[action]).squeeze(0)
        loss=F.mse_loss(prediction,after)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(net.parameters(),1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        if step==1 or step%max(1,steps//20)==0 or step==steps:
            history.append({'step':step,'transition_mse':float(loss.detach())})
    checkpoint=directory/'world_model.pt'
    torch.save({'model':net.state_dict(),'hidden':hidden,'steps':steps,'seed':seed},checkpoint)
    result={'steps':steps,'training_duration_sec':time.monotonic()-started,
            'last_transition_mse':history[-1]['transition_mse'],
            'checkpoint':str(checkpoint),'scope':'synthetic DSL feature transitions'}
    (directory/'world_training.json').write_text(json.dumps({'result':result,'history':history},indent=2))
    return result


def load_world_model(path):
    bundle=torch.load(path,map_location='cpu',weights_only=True)
    model=HypothesisWorldModel(bundle['hidden'])
    model.load_state_dict(bundle['model'])
    model.eval()
    return model

"""Evaluate three ALREADY trained widths without rerunning 1000 training steps.

All sizes see the same public few-shot tasks. Hidden examples are accessed
only after the search completes. Avoid touching test examples in memory.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
import argparse,csv,json,random,statistics,time
from nexus_m.learning import restore_checkpoint
from nexus_m.memory import PersistentMemory
from nexus_m.benchmark import evaluate_configuration,aggregate,write_csv,plot_summary
from nexus_m.tasks import sample_task

p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
p.add_argument('--root',default='outputs')
p.add_argument('--output',default='outputs/comparacao_treinados')
p.add_argument('--widths',nargs='+',type=int,default=[100,1000,10000])
p.add_argument('--seeds',nargs='+',type=int,default=[777,778,779])
p.add_argument('--episodes',type=int,default=12)
p.add_argument('--simulations',type=int,default=300)
p.add_argument('--depth',type=int,default=32)
a=p.parse_args();root=Path(a.root);out=Path(a.output);out.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
all_rows=[];summary=[]
for seed in a.seeds:
    rng=random.Random(seed)
    task_list=[sample_task(rng,'ood',5,20) for _ in range(a.episodes)]
    for width in a.widths:
        folder=root/f'real_100x{width}_1000'
        model,bundle=restore_checkpoint(folder/'model_final.pt')
        model.eval()
        with PersistentMemory(folder/'memory.sqlite') as memory:
            conditions=[('neural_collective_memory',True,True,True),
                        ('neural_collective_no_memory',True,True,False),
                        ('neural_isolated',False,True,False),
                        ('uniform_mcts',False,False,False)]
            for name,comm,neural,mem in conditions:
                rows=evaluate_configuration(model,memory,task_list,simulations=a.simulations,
                     max_depth=a.depth,seed=seed,communication=comm,neural=neural,
                     use_memory=mem,telemetry=None)
                for i,row in enumerate(rows):
                    all_rows.append({'seed':seed,'width':width,'agents':100,'scenario':name,
                                     'task_index':i,**row})
                ag=aggregate(rows)
                summary.append({'seed':seed,'width':width,'agents':100,
                                'neurons':model.neuron_count,
                                'parameters':model.report()['trainable_parameters'],
                                'scenario':name,'train_sec':0.,**ag})
                print('width=',width,'seed=',seed,'scenario=',name,
                      'ood_exact=',round(ag['ood_exact'],3),'ood_accuracy=',round(ag['ood_accuracy'],3),flush=True)
            write_csv(out/'results_by_task.csv',all_rows)
            write_csv(out/'summary.csv',summary)
(out/'summary.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
plot_summary(out/'summary.csv',out/'comparison.png')
print('Saved',out)


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: scripts/benchmark_evolution.py
SHA256: 8cc05fec9ad4351cb9e74780e1ac3478bafb520cbdfe53cc35237bfba86ea9c2
